===== FILE backend/tests/test_prompt_contract_merge.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy

import pytest

from app.audit import canonical_json


def _contract(name: str, properties: dict, required: list[str] | None = None) -> dict:
    return {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": name,
        "description": name,
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": properties,
            "required": required if required is not None else list(properties),
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }


def _assert_closed(schema: dict) -> None:
    if schema.get("type") == "object":
        assert schema["additionalProperties"] is False
        assert schema["required"] == sorted(schema["properties"])
        for child in schema["properties"].values():
            _assert_closed(child)
    elif schema.get("type") == "array":
        _assert_closed(schema["items"])


def test_merge_keeps_all_fixed_fields_and_closes_every_object() -> None:
    from app.prompt_contract_merge import merge_strict_prompt_contracts

    first = _contract(
        "call_a",
        {
            "classification": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {"category": {"type": "string"}},
                "required": ["category"],
                "additionalProperties": False,
            },
            "score": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100},
        },
    )
    second = _contract(
        "call_b",
        {
            "classification": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {"style": {"type": "string"}},
                "required": ["style"],
                "additionalProperties": False,
            },
            "dimensions": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {
                        "name": {"type": "string"},
                        "grade": {"type": "integer"},
                    },
                    "required": ["name", "grade"],
                    "additionalProperties": False,
                },
            },
        },
    )

    merged = merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
        [first, second],
        name="combined_result",
        description="A 与 B 的固定字段并集",
    )

    assert list(merged["schema"]["properties"]) == [
        "classification",
        "dimensions",
        "score",
    ]
    assert list(merged["schema"]["properties"]["classification"]["properties"]) == [
        "category",
        "style",
    ]
    _assert_closed(merged["schema"])


def test_merge_rejects_same_path_with_different_types() -> None:
    from app.prompt_contract_merge import (
        PromptContractMergeError,
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts,
    )

    with pytest.raises(PromptContractMergeError) as caught:
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
            [
                _contract("a", {"score": {"type": "integer"}}),
                _contract("b", {"score": {"type": "string"}}),
            ],
            name="combined",
        )

    assert caught.value.code == "prompt_contract_field_conflict"
    assert caught.value.path == "$.score"


def test_merge_rejects_same_path_with_different_enum_semantics() -> None:
    from app.prompt_contract_merge import (
        PromptContractMergeError,
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts,
    )

    with pytest.raises(PromptContractMergeError) as caught:
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
            [
                _contract(
                    "a",
                    {"level": {"type": "string", "enum": ["L1", "L2"]}},
                ),
                _contract(
                    "b",
                    {"level": {"type": "string", "enum": ["L1", "L3"]}},
                ),
            ],
            name="combined",
        )

    assert caught.value.code == "prompt_contract_field_conflict"
    assert caught.value.path == "$.level"


def test_merge_hash_is_independent_of_input_contract_order() -> None:
    from app.prompt_contract_merge import (
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts,
        prompt_contract_hash,
    )

    first = _contract("a", {"alpha": {"type": "string"}})
    second = _contract("b", {"beta": {"type": "integer"}})
    ab = merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
        [first, second],
        name="combined",
        description="fixed",
    )
    ba = merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
        [deepcopy(second), deepcopy(first)],
        name="combined",
        description="fixed",
    )

    assert canonical_json(ab) == canonical_json(ba)
    assert prompt_contract_hash(ab) == prompt_contract_hash(ba)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "mutation",
    [
        {"mode": "json_object"},
        {"strict": False},
        {"schema": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}},
    ],
)
def test_merge_rejects_non_strict_or_non_object_contracts(mutation: dict) -> None:
    from app.prompt_contract_merge import (
        PromptContractMergeError,
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts,
    )

    contract = _contract("a", {"score": {"type": "integer"}})
    contract.update(mutation)

    with pytest.raises(PromptContractMergeError) as caught:
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts([contract], name="combined")

    assert caught.value.code == "prompt_contract_invalid"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_prompt_kg_rejection_evidence.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
from app.prompt_output_contract import PromptOutputContract
from app.workflow_model_executor import (
    WorkflowModelExecutorError,
    real_prompt_node_result,
)


def _overseas_single(color: str) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "res_id": "asset-77",
        "is_single": 2,
        "title": "Modern Oak Chair",
        "space": "",
        "object": "chair",
        "style": "modern minimal",
        "material": "wood",
        "structural_features": "with arms",
        "architectural_element": "",
        "soft_decoration": "",
        "hard_decoration": "",
        "color": color,
        "form": "geometric",
        "structure": "single",
    }


def test_prompt_step_kg_rejection_keeps_model_output_as_evidence() -> None:
    """提示词节点校验失败时，被拒的模型输出必须随异常带出去落 checkpoint。

    run159 九条失败全是「color 最多允许 1 个值」，可失败点在提示词节点，
    昨天只给投影节点挂了证据，排查时仍然看不到模型给了哪几个颜色。
    """

    client_config = SimpleNamespace(
        thinking_mode="disabled",
        provider="doubao",
        model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428",
        base_url="https://ark.example/api/v3",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
        temperature=0.1,
        max_retries=1,
        timeout_seconds=120,
        name="主评测模型",
    )
    prepared = SimpleNamespace(
        output_contract=PromptOutputContract(mode="none"),
        output_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.single.v1",
        expected_asset_id="asset-77",
        node_instance_id="prompt_overseas_single",
        invocation_key="root",
        component_hash="c" * 64,
        model_snapshot_hash="m" * 64,
        output_budget=4096,
        reasoning_effort=None,
        client_config=client_config,
    )
    response = DoubaoResponse(
        parsed=_overseas_single("white,black"),
        raw_text="{}",
        raw_payload={},
        output_budget=4096,
        reasoning_effort=None,
        thinking_mode="disabled",
        input_tokens=1200,
        output_tokens=180,
        total_tokens=1380,
        input_image_bytes=1024,
        input_image_tokens=600,
    )

    with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as captured:
        real_prompt_node_result(prepared, response)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    assert captured.value.code == "kg_output_value_invalid"
    assert "color" in str(captured.value)
    evidence = captured.value.evidence
    assert evidence["contract_key"] == "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.single.v1"
    assert evidence["rejected_model_output"]["color"] == "white,black"
    assert evidence["rejected_model_output"]["res_id"] == "asset-77"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_prompt_lab.py =====
"""提示词实验室（给运营 Codex 的受限接口）：只改提示词、只跑开放类目、令牌独立、并发与每日上限。"""
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import get_knowledge_graph_contract
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    AuditEvent,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    PromptVersion,
    WorkflowVersion,
)

TOKEN = "t" * 40


def test_candidate_api_can_freeze_additive_output_fields(lab):
    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    node = next(n for n in source["prompt_nodes"] if n["node_id"] == "prompt_overseas_single")
    from copy import deepcopy
    schema = deepcopy(node["output_contract"]["output_schema"])
    schema["properties"]["review_required"] = {"type": "boolean"}
    schema["properties"]["view_angle"] = {"type": "string", "enum": ["front", ""]}
    schema["required"] += ["review_required", "view_angle"]
    body = {"source_version_id": source["id"], "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"],
            "prompts": {}, "output_schemas": {node["node_id"]: schema}}
    response = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
    result = response.json()
    frozen = next(n for n in result["mechanism"]["prompt_nodes"] if n["node_id"] == node["node_id"])
    assert frozen["output_contract"]["output_schema"] == schema
    assert frozen["output_contract"]["contract_hash"] != node["output_contract"]["contract_hash"]
    assert lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body).json()["candidate_version_id"] == result["candidate_version_id"]
    run = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": result["candidate_version_id"], "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline})
    assert run.status_code == 201, run.text
    lab.drain()
    assert lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{run.json()['id']}").json()["status"] == "completed"
    items = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{run.json()['id']}/items").json()["items"]
    overseas = next(item for item in items if "overseas" in item["truth_contract_key"])
    assert overseas["prediction"]["review_required"] is False
    assert "view_angle" in overseas["prediction"]
    assert any(v["contract_hash"] == frozen["output_contract"]["contract_hash"] for v in overseas["output_contract_versions"])
    original = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{source['id']}").json()
    assert original == source


def test_candidate_api_rejects_unknown_permissions_and_identity_change(lab):
    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    body = {"source_version_id": source["id"], "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"], "prompts": {}, "activate": True}
    assert lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body).status_code == 422
    body.pop("activate")
    node = source["prompt_nodes"][0]
    schema = node["output_contract"]["output_schema"]
    schema["properties"]["is_single"]["enum"] = [99]
    body["output_schemas"] = {node["node_id"]: schema}
    response = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert response.status_code == 422, response.text
    assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "kg_schema_protected_field"


@pytest.fixture()
def lab(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    from app.seed import seed_defaults
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow
    import app.prompt_lab as prompt_lab
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.prompt_lab_api import build_prompt_lab_router

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'lab.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
    monkeypatch.setattr(prompt_lab, "MODEL_EXECUTION_MODE", "simulated")
    with sessions() as db:
        seed_defaults(db)
        workflow = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(db, actor="test", category_key="knowledge_graph")
        baseline = BaselineSet(category_key="knowledge_graph", name="kg lab", description="", default_expected_level="L1", fingerprint="b" * 64, created_by="test")
        db.add(baseline); db.flush()
        item_ids = []
        for n, (site, scope) in enumerate([("domestic", "whole"), ("overseas", "single")]):
            contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(f"kg.knowledge_graph.{site}.{scope}.v1")
            asset = Asset(original_name=f"{site}-{scope}.jpg", stored_name=f"{n}.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=100, sha256=f"{n}" * 64, category_key="knowledge_graph")
            db.add(asset); db.flush()
            truth = {field: "" for field in contract.field_order}
            truth.update({contract.identity_field: f"asset-{n}", "is_single": contract.is_single, "color": "gray"})
            item = BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=baseline.id, asset_id=asset.id, expected_level=None, expected_result_json=canonical_json(truth),
                truth_contract_key=contract.contract_key,
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json({"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256, "workflow_input": {
                    "source": site, "is_single": contract.is_single, contract.identity_field: f"asset-{n}", "fixture_key": f"kg.{site}.{scope}.v1"}}))
            db.add(item); db.flush()
            item_ids.append(item.id)
        other = Asset(original_name="x.jpg", stored_name="x.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=1, sha256="f" * 64, category_key="model_3d_su")
        db.add(other)
        db.commit()
        ids = SimpleNamespace(workflow=workflow.id, workflow_hash=workflow.canonical_hash, baseline=baseline.id, items=item_ids, other_asset=other.id)

    settings = SimpleNamespace(prompt_lab_token=TOKEN)
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_prompt_lab_router(lambda: settings))

    def db_override():
        db = sessions()
        try:
            yield db
        finally:
            db.close()

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override
    client = TestClient(app)
    client.headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {TOKEN}"

    def drain():
        while executor.process_runtime_step_once("lab-test"):
            pass

    yield SimpleNamespace(client=client, sessions=sessions, ids=ids, settings=settings, drain=drain)
    engine.dispose()


def test_token_is_separate_and_required(lab):
    assert lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/guide").status_code == 200
    anonymous = TestClient(lab.client.app)
    assert anonymous.get("/api/prompt-lab/capabilities").status_code == 401
    permissions = lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/capabilities").json()
    assert "candidates:output_schema_write" in permissions["permissions"]
    assert permissions["production_activation"] is False
    assert anonymous.get("/api/prompt-lab/guide").status_code == 401
    assert anonymous.get("/api/prompt-lab/guide", headers={"Authorization": "Bearer " + "x" * 40}).status_code == 401
    lab.settings.prompt_lab_token = "short"
    assert lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/guide").status_code == 503
    lab.settings.prompt_lab_token = None
    assert lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/guide").status_code == 503


def test_candidate_changes_only_prompt_text_and_is_idempotent(lab):
    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    nodes = {n["node_id"]: n for n in source["prompt_nodes"]}
    assert {"prompt_domestic_whole", "prompt_overseas_single"} <= set(nodes)
    target = "prompt_overseas_single"
    body = {"source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"],
            "prompts": {target: {"system_prompt": nodes[target]["system_prompt"] + "\nAlways answer in English."}}, "note": "english only"}
    first = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert first.status_code == 201, first.text
    receipt = first.json()
    assert receipt["created"] is True and receipt["changed_nodes"] == [target]
    candidate = receipt["mechanism"]
    assert candidate["is_lab_candidate"] is True and candidate["status"] == "active"
    after = {n["node_id"]: n for n in candidate["prompt_nodes"]}
    assert after[target]["system_prompt"].endswith("Always answer in English.")
    assert after[target]["user_prompt"] == nodes[target]["user_prompt"]
    for node_id, node in nodes.items():
        if node_id != target:
            assert after[node_id]["system_prompt"] == node["system_prompt"]
            assert after[node_id]["prompt_version_id"] == node["prompt_version_id"]
        assert after[node_id]["output_contract"] == node["output_contract"]
    again = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body).json()
    assert again["candidate_version_id"] == receipt["candidate_version_id"] and again["created"] is False
    listed = lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms").json()["items"]
    assert {row["id"] for row in listed} >= {lab.ids.workflow, receipt["candidate_version_id"]}
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.category == "prompt_lab", AuditEvent.action == "candidate_created")) is not None
    # 候选能直接跑回归。
    run = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": receipt["candidate_version_id"], "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline}).json()
    lab.drain()
    assert lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{run['id']}").json()["status"] == "completed"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("change,status,code", [
    ({"prompt_nope": {"system_prompt": "x"}}, 422, "prompt_node_unknown"),
    ({"prompt_domestic_whole": {"system_prompt": "输出 {{SECRET_ENV}}"}}, 422, "prompt_placeholder_not_allowed"),
    ({"prompt_domestic_whole": {"system_prompt": "   "}}, 422, "prompt_text_empty"),
])
def test_candidate_rejects_bad_edits(lab, change, status, code):
    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    response = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json={
        "source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"], "prompts": change})
    assert response.status_code == status and response.json()["detail"]["code"] == code
    stale = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json={
        "source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": "0" * 64, "prompts": {"prompt_domestic_whole": {"system_prompt": "x"}}})
    assert stale.status_code == 409 and stale.json()["detail"]["code"] == "source_hash_mismatch"
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion).where(WorkflowVersion.created_by == "prompt-lab:codex")) is None


def test_run_limits_items_and_cancel(lab, monkeypatch):
    c = lab.client
    first = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline,
                                                 "baseline_item_ids": [lab.ids.items[0]], "client_request_id": "req-00000001"})
    assert first.status_code == 201, first.text
    run = first.json()
    assert run["is_lab_run"] is True and run["total"] == 1 and run["status"] == "running"
    replay = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline,
                                                  "baseline_item_ids": [lab.ids.items[0]], "client_request_id": "req-00000001"})
    assert replay.json()["id"] == run["id"] and replay.json()["created"] is False
    busy = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline})
    assert busy.status_code == 409 and busy.json()["detail"]["code"] == "lab_run_in_progress"
    lab.drain()
    done = c.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{run['id']}").json()
    assert done["status"] == "completed"
    page = c.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{run['id']}/items").json()
    assert page["total"] == 1
    row = page["items"][0]
    assert row["baseline_set_item_id"] == lab.ids.items[0] and row["file_name"] == "domestic-whole.jpg"
    assert row["truth_at_run"]["color"] == "gray" and row["current_truth"]["expected_result"]["color"] == "gray"
    assert isinstance(row["prediction"], dict) and isinstance(row["raw_output"], dict)
    # 每日上限（东八区自然日）按实验室已跑条数算。
    monkeypatch.setenv("PROMPT_LAB_DAILY_ITEM_CAP", "2")
    capped = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline})
    assert capped.status_code == 429 and capped.json()["detail"]["code"] == "lab_daily_cap_reached"
    monkeypatch.delenv("PROMPT_LAB_DAILY_ITEM_CAP")
    second = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline}).json()
    cancelled = c.post(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{second['id']}/cancel")
    assert cancelled.status_code == 200 and cancelled.json()["status"] == "failed"
    assert [r["id"] for r in c.get("/api/prompt-lab/runs?lab_only=true").json()["items"]] == [second["id"], run["id"]]


def test_cannot_cancel_or_read_outside_the_lab_boundary(lab):
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.prompt_lab import PromptLabError, require_asset

    with lab.sessions() as db:
        operator_run = create_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_set=db.get(BaselineSet, lab.ids.baseline),
            workflow_version=db.get(WorkflowVersion, lab.ids.workflow), actor="liangjx", idempotency_key="page-run")
        db.commit()
        operator_run_id = operator_run.id
        with pytest.raises(PromptLabError) as denied:
            require_asset(db, lab.ids.other_asset)
        assert denied.value.status_code == 403
        kg_asset = db.get(BaselineSetItem, lab.ids.items[0]).asset_id
        assert require_asset(db, kg_asset).id == kg_asset
    refused = lab.client.post(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{operator_run_id}/cancel")
    assert refused.status_code == 403 and refused.json()["detail"]["code"] == "run_not_owned"
    # 运营在页面上跑的回归只读可见（用来读纠偏轮），不能取消。
    assert lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{operator_run_id}").json()["is_lab_run"] is False
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, operator_run_id).status == "running"


def test_pricing_update_after_source_freeze_does_not_block_candidates(lab, monkeypatch):
    """.137 实测：价目表升级后重新冻结老机制只有计费快照不同，不能因此拒绝候选；模型本身变了仍要拒绝。"""
    import app.workflow_v3 as workflow_v3
    from app.models import ModelConfig, WorkflowVersion
    from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest

    original = workflow_v3.build_model_config_snapshot

    def repriced(config):
        snapshot = original(config)
        snapshot["pricing_contract"] = {"schema_version": "model-pricing-v3", "source_url": "https://example.invalid/v3"}
        return snapshot

    monkeypatch.setattr(workflow_v3, "build_model_config_snapshot", repriced)
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(db.get(WorkflowVersion, lab.ids.workflow))
        node = next(n for n in manifest["nodes"] if "prompt_version_id" in (n.get("config") or {}))
        config = db.get(ModelConfig, node["config"]["model_snapshot"]["source_model_config_id"])
    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    assert all(n["model"]["provider"] for n in source["prompt_nodes"])
    target = source["prompt_nodes"][0]
    body = {"source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"],
            "prompts": {target["node_id"]: {"system_prompt": target["system_prompt"] + "\n补一句", "user_prompt": ""}}}
    created = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
    assert next(n for n in created.json()["mechanism"]["prompt_nodes"] if n["node_id"] == target["node_id"])["user_prompt"] == ""
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        frozen = persisted_workflow_manifest(db.get(WorkflowVersion, created.json()["candidate_version_id"]))
        assert all(n["config"]["model_snapshot"]["runtime_config"]["pricing_contract"]["schema_version"] == "model-pricing-v3"
                   for n in frozen["nodes"] if "prompt_version_id" in (n.get("config") or {}))
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        config = db.get(ModelConfig, config.id)
        config.name = "renamed-model-config"
        db.commit()
    body["prompts"][target["node_id"]]["system_prompt"] += "再补一句"
    changed = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert changed.status_code == 409 and changed.json()["detail"]["code"] == "candidate_differs_beyond_prompts"
    # 模型换成冻结不了的型号：返回 409 并说明原因，不是 500，也不留下孤儿提示词版本。
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        before_prompts = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(PromptVersion))
        config = db.get(ModelConfig, config.id)
        config.model_id = "some-unknown-vision-model"
        db.commit()
    body["prompts"][target["node_id"]]["system_prompt"] += "第三句"
    unfreezable = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert unfreezable.status_code == 409, unfreezable.text
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(PromptVersion)) == before_prompts


def _second_set(lab):
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        second = BaselineSet(category_key="knowledge_graph", name="kg lab 2", description="", default_expected_level="L1", fingerprint="c" * 64, created_by="test")
        db.add(second); db.flush()
        src = db.get(BaselineSetItem, lab.ids.items[1])
        db.add(BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=second.id, asset_id=src.asset_id, expected_level=None, expected_result_json=src.expected_result_json,
                               truth_contract_key=src.truth_contract_key, asset_snapshot_json=src.asset_snapshot_json))
        db.commit()
        return second.id


def test_concurrent_run_requests_on_different_sets_are_serialized(lab, monkeypatch):
    """ls-review-r23 R1：两个请求各选一个基准集同时进来，不能都读到"没有在跑"而各建一轮。"""
    import threading
    import time
    import app.native_baseline_regression as nbr
    from app.prompt_lab import LabWriteLock, PromptLabError, start_run

    second_id = _second_set(lab)
    original = nbr.create_native_baseline_run
    entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()

    def slow(*args, **kwargs):
        entered.set()
        release.wait(10)
        return original(*args, **kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(nbr, "create_native_baseline_run", slow)
    results = {}

    def attempt(name, set_id):
        with lab.sessions() as db:
            try:
                with LabWriteLock(db):
                    out = start_run(db, workflow_version_id=lab.ids.workflow, baseline_set_id=set_id)
                    db.commit()
                results[name] = out["id"]
            except PromptLabError as exc:
                db.rollback()
                results[name] = exc.code

    first = threading.Thread(target=attempt, args=("a", lab.ids.baseline)); first.start()
    assert entered.wait(10)
    second = threading.Thread(target=attempt, args=("b", second_id)); second.start()
    time.sleep(0.5)
    assert "b" not in results  # 第二个请求在锁外等第一个提交
    release.set(); first.join(20); second.join(20)
    assert isinstance(results["a"], int) and results["b"] == "lab_run_in_progress"
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(BaselineRegressionRun).where(BaselineRegressionRun.created_by == "prompt-lab:codex")) == 1


def test_client_request_id_only_replays_the_identical_request(lab):
    """ls-review-r23 R2：同一个 client_request_id 换了条目或基准集，必须 409，不能返回没跑这些样本的旧轮次。"""
    c = lab.client
    base = {"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline, "client_request_id": "req-same-0001"}
    first = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={**base, "baseline_item_ids": [lab.ids.items[0]]})
    assert first.status_code == 201
    same = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={**base, "baseline_item_ids": [lab.ids.items[0]]})
    assert same.json()["id"] == first.json()["id"] and same.json()["created"] is False
    other_items = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={**base, "baseline_item_ids": [lab.ids.items[1]]})
    assert other_items.status_code == 409 and other_items.json()["detail"]["code"] == "client_request_id_reused"
    lab.drain()
    other_set = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={**base, "baseline_set_id": _second_set(lab)})
    assert other_set.status_code == 409 and other_set.json()["detail"]["code"] == "client_request_id_reused"


def test_run_items_page_does_not_query_per_item(lab):
    """ls-review-r23 S2：逐条结果按页批量取，查询次数不随每页条数增长。"""
    from sqlalchemy import event
    from app.prompt_lab import run_items

    run = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline}).json()
    lab.drain()
    counts = {}
    for limit in (1, 2):
        with lab.sessions() as db:
            statements = []
            listener = lambda conn, cursor, statement, *rest: statements.append(statement)
            engine = db.get_bind()
            event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", listener)
            try:
                page = run_items(db, run["id"], limit=limit)
            finally:
                event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", listener)
            assert len(page["items"]) == limit and all("expected_result" in row["current_truth"] for row in page["items"])
            counts[limit] = sum(1 for s in statements if s.lstrip().upper().startswith("SELECT"))
    assert counts[1] == counts[2], counts


class _FakeConn:
    def __init__(self, log, result=1, fail=False):
        self.log, self.result, self.fail = log, result, fail

    def execute(self, statement, params=None):
        self.log.append(str(statement).split("(")[0].replace("SELECT ", ""))
        if self.fail and "GET_LOCK" in str(statement):
            raise RuntimeError("lost connection mid-query")
        return SimpleNamespace(scalar=lambda: self.result)

    def invalidate(self):
        self.log.append("invalidate")

    def close(self):
        self.log.append("close")


def _fake_mysql_lock(monkeypatch, conn):
    import app.prompt_lab as prompt_lab

    business = SimpleNamespace(dialect=SimpleNamespace(name="mysql"))
    lock_channel = SimpleNamespace(connect=lambda: conn)
    monkeypatch.setattr(prompt_lab, "_lock_engine", lambda engine: lock_channel if engine is business else None)
    db = SimpleNamespace(in_transaction=lambda: False, get_bind=lambda: business)
    return prompt_lab.LabWriteLock(db)


def test_mysql_named_lock_uses_its_own_channel_and_always_cleans_up(monkeypatch):
    """ls-review-r24 F1/F3：锁连接走独立通道；拿到锁时退出先 RELEASE_LOCK 再断开；
    拿不到返回 409；GET_LOCK 出异常时作废并断开，不把可能持锁的会话留着。"""
    from app.prompt_lab import PromptLabError

    log = []
    with _fake_mysql_lock(monkeypatch, _FakeConn(log)):
        log.append("action+commit")
    assert log == ["GET_LOCK", "action+commit", "RELEASE_LOCK", "close"]
    log = []
    with pytest.raises(PromptLabError) as busy:
        _fake_mysql_lock(monkeypatch, _FakeConn(log, result=0)).__enter__()
    assert busy.value.code == "lab_busy" and busy.value.status_code == 409 and log == ["GET_LOCK", "close"]
    log = []
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        _fake_mysql_lock(monkeypatch, _FakeConn(log, fail=True)).__enter__()
    assert log == ["GET_LOCK", "invalidate", "close"]


def test_lock_engine_is_outside_the_business_pool():
    from sqlalchemy.pool import NullPool
    from app.prompt_lab import _lock_engine

    business = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    channel = _lock_engine(business)
    assert channel is not business and isinstance(channel.pool, NullPool) and _lock_engine(business) is channel


def test_mechanism_list_is_bounded_by_the_requested_page(lab):
    """Astra 全仓复审 F1：limit=1 不能把全部机制版本（含其他类目、大正文）都载入。"""
    from sqlalchemy import event
    from app.prompt_lab import list_mechanisms

    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    node = source["prompt_nodes"][0]
    for i in range(3):
        created = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json={
            "source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"],
            "prompts": {node["node_id"]: {"system_prompt": node["system_prompt"] + f"\n版本 {i}"}}})
        assert created.status_code == 201
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        loaded = []
        listener = lambda session, instance: loaded.append(instance)
        event.listen(db, "loaded_as_persistent", listener)
        try:
            page = list_mechanisms(db, limit=1)
        finally:
            event.remove(db, "loaded_as_persistent", listener)
        assert len(page) == 1 and page[0]["is_lab_candidate"] is True
        versions = [obj for obj in loaded if isinstance(obj, WorkflowVersion)]
        assert len(versions) == 1, len(versions)
        listed = list_mechanisms(db, limit=10)
        assert len([v for v in listed if v["category_key"] == "knowledge_graph"]) == 4
        assert any(v["category_key"] != "knowledge_graph" for v in listed)


def test_mechanism_list_reports_scan_cap_instead_of_going_silent(lab, monkeypatch):
    """Astra 复审 N1：定义允许多个类目时，冻结类目不开放的版本会被跳过；扫满上限还没凑够条数时，
    旧实现静默返回空列表且上限在页外检查。现在页内守住上限，返回 scan_capped 与 next_before_id 可续翻。"""
    import app.prompt_lab as prompt_lab
    from sqlalchemy import event

    # Keep this scan-cap probe scoped to one category; the public API now exposes all registered categories.
    monkeypatch.setattr(prompt_lab, "allowed_categories", lambda db: frozenset({"knowledge_graph"}))

    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    node = source["prompt_nodes"][0]
    candidate_ids = []
    for i in range(3):
        created = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json={
            "source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"],
            "prompts": {node["node_id"]: {"system_prompt": node["system_prompt"] + f"\n扫描上限 {i}"}}})
        assert created.status_code == 201
        candidate_ids.append(created.json()["candidate_version_id"])
    # 模拟同一定义下其它类目的版本：最新的三个候选冻结类目不在开放范围。
    foreign = set(candidate_ids)
    real_manifest = prompt_lab._manifest
    monkeypatch.setattr(prompt_lab, "_manifest", lambda version: (
        {"category_key": "space_image"} if version.id in foreign else real_manifest(version)))

    with lab.sessions() as db:
        loaded = []
        listener = lambda session, instance: loaded.append(instance)
        event.listen(db, "loaded_as_persistent", listener)
        try:
            first = prompt_lab.list_mechanisms_page(db, limit=5, scan_cap=2)
        finally:
            event.remove(db, "loaded_as_persistent", listener)
        assert first["items"] == [] and first["scan_capped"] is True and first["scanned"] == 2
        assert first["next_before_id"] == sorted(candidate_ids)[1]
        assert len([obj for obj in loaded if isinstance(obj, WorkflowVersion)]) == 2  # 页内守住上限
        rest = prompt_lab.list_mechanisms_page(db, limit=5, scan_cap=2, before_id=first["next_before_id"])
        assert [item["id"] for item in rest["items"]] == [lab.ids.workflow]
        # 刚好扫完：没有更早的版本，不误报不完整。
        assert rest["scan_capped"] is False and rest["next_before_id"] is None

    listed = lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms", params={"limit": 1})
    assert listed.status_code == 200
    body = listed.json()
    assert set(body) >= {"items", "next_before_id", "scan_capped"}
    assert [item["id"] for item in body["items"]] == [lab.ids.workflow]
    assert body["next_before_id"] is None and body["scan_capped"] is False

===== FILE backend/tests/test_prompt_loader.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from app.prompt_loader import load_prompt_pairs, load_standalone_prompt


PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def test_v21_prompt_is_split_into_two_complete_calls() -> None:
    pairs = load_prompt_pairs(PROJECT_ROOT / "prompts" / "3d66-aesthetic-v2.1.md")
    assert set(pairs) == {"A", "B"}
    assert len(pairs["A"].system) > 2000
    assert len(pairs["B"].system) > 2000
    assert "{{image_metadata}}" in pairs["A"].user
    assert "{{precheck_json}}" in pairs["B"].user
    assert "{{rubric_version}}" in pairs["B"].user


def test_v13_split_prompts_match_runtime_contract() -> None:
    prompt_a = load_standalone_prompt(
        PROJECT_ROOT / "prompts" / "space-precheck-v1.3-split.1.md"
    )
    prompt_b = load_standalone_prompt(
        PROJECT_ROOT / "prompts" / "space-aesthetic-dimensions-v1.3-split.1.md"
    )
    assert len(prompt_a.system) > 4000
    assert "scope_status" in prompt_a.system
    assert "{{image_metadata}}" in prompt_a.user
    assert len(prompt_b.system) > 5000
    assert '"scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3"' in prompt_b.system
    assert "{{precheck_json}}" in prompt_b.user
    assert "{{rubric_version}}" in prompt_b.user
