def test_health_snapshot_redacts_database_failure() -> None:
    main_module = __import__("app.main", fromlist=["_runtime_health_snapshot"])

    class BrokenDatabase:
        def connection(self):
            raise OperationalError(
                "SELECT 1",
                {},
                sqlite3.OperationalError(
                    "database is locked password=hidden"
                ),
            )

    snapshot = main_module._runtime_health_snapshot(BrokenDatabase())

    assert snapshot["status"] == "degraded"
    assert snapshot["database"] == {"status": "unavailable"}
    serialized = json.dumps(snapshot)
    assert "password" not in serialized
    assert "database is locked" not in serialized


def test_fifo_is_preserved_inside_selected_queue() -> None:
    scheduler = DeterministicQueueScheduler(QueuePolicy(global_limit=3))
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    selected = scheduler.choose_job(
        [
            QueueJob(3, "interactive", now),
            QueueJob(1, "interactive", now),
            QueueJob(2, "interactive", now),
        ],
        running={},
    )
    assert selected is not None
    assert selected.id == 1


def test_idle_capacity_is_borrowed_and_reclaimed_without_preemption() -> None:
    policy = QueuePolicy(global_limit=10)
    borrowed = queue_capacities(
        policy,
        pending={
            "validation": 0,
            "interactive": 10,
            "production_batch": 0,
            "canary": 0,
            "recovery": 0,
        },
        running={
            "validation": 0,
            "interactive": 5,
            "production_batch": 0,
            "canary": 0,
            "recovery": 0,
        },
    )
    assert borrowed["interactive"].borrowed > 0
    assert borrowed["interactive"].effective_limit == 10

    scheduler = DeterministicQueueScheduler(policy)
    selected = scheduler.choose_queue(
        pending={
            "validation": 1,
            "interactive": 10,
            "production_batch": 0,
            "canary": 0,
            "recovery": 0,
        },
        running={
            "validation": 0,
            "interactive": 5,
            "production_batch": 0,
            "canary": 0,
            "recovery": 0,
        },
    )
    assert selected == "validation"


def test_validation_boost_recovery_limit_and_long_term_fairness() -> None:
    policy = QueuePolicy(global_limit=1)
    scheduler = DeterministicQueueScheduler(policy)
    counts: Counter[str] = Counter()
    pending = {
        "validation": 10000,
        "interactive": 10000,
        "production_batch": 10000,
        "canary": 10000,
        "recovery": 10000,
    }
    for _ in range(500):
        selected = scheduler.choose_queue(pending=pending, running={})
        assert selected is not None
        counts[selected] += 1
    assert set(counts) == set(pending)
    assert all(counts[queue] > 0 for queue in pending)
    assert counts["production_batch"] > max(
        count
        for queue, count in counts.items()
        if queue != "production_batch"
    )
    assert counts["validation"] > 500 * DEFAULT_SHARES["validation"] / 100

    boosted = DeterministicQueueScheduler(
        QueuePolicy(global_limit=1, validation_boost=60)
    )
    boosted_counts: Counter[str] = Counter()
    for _ in range(500):
        selected = boosted.choose_queue(pending=pending, running={})
        assert selected is not None
        boosted_counts[selected] += 1
    assert boosted_counts["validation"] > boosted_counts["production_batch"]

    capacities = queue_capacities(
        QueuePolicy(global_limit=20),
        pending={"recovery": 100},
        running={"recovery": 0},
    )
    # Every fresh lane idle: recovery may borrow all of their idle reserved
    # capacity (2026-09-12: a 2x-share cap stranded 109 ready batch retries).
    assert capacities["recovery"].effective_limit == 20
    assert queue_capacities(QueuePolicy(global_limit=20), pending={"recovery": 100, "validation": 1},
                            running={"recovery": 0})["recovery"].effective_limit == 1


def test_scheduler_state_survives_reconstruction_deterministically() -> None:
    policy = QueuePolicy(global_limit=3)
    pending = {queue: 100 for queue in DEFAULT_SHARES}
    continuous = DeterministicQueueScheduler(policy)
    expected = [
        continuous.choose_queue(pending=pending, running={})
        for _ in range(40)
    ]

    first = DeterministicQueueScheduler(policy)
    actual = [
        first.choose_queue(pending=pending, running={})
        for _ in range(13)
    ]
    state = first.export_state()
    rebuilt = DeterministicQueueScheduler(
        policy,
        deficits=state["deficits"],
        dispatch_count=state["dispatch_count"],
        last_recovery_dispatch=state["last_recovery_dispatch"],
    )
    actual.extend(
        rebuilt.choose_queue(pending=pending, running={})
        for _ in range(27)
    )
    assert actual == expected


def test_global_limit_blocks_dispatch() -> None:
    scheduler = DeterministicQueueScheduler(QueuePolicy(global_limit=2))
    selected = scheduler.choose_queue(
        pending={"interactive": 1},
        running={"validation": 1, "production_batch": 1},
    )
    assert selected is None


def test_retry_classification_budget_backoff_and_retry_after() -> None:
    timeout = classify_technical_failure(TimeoutError("timed out"))
    assert timeout.retryable is True
    assert timeout.error_type == "timeout"
    assert classify_technical_failure(
        "provider error", status_code=503
    ).error_type == "provider5xx"
    assert classify_technical_failure(
        "rate limit", status_code=429, retry_after_seconds=7
    ).retry_after_seconds == 7
    non_retryable = classify_technical_failure("invalid credentials")
    assert non_retryable.retryable is False
    assert non_retryable.priority == "P0"
    assert (
        classify_technical_failure(
            "business result has low confidence and parse conflict"
        ).retryable
        is False
    )
    http_429 = worker._technical_failure_from_exception(
        DoubaoHTTPError(429, {"Retry-After": "11"})
    )
    assert http_429.error_type == "429"
    assert http_429.retry_after_seconds == 11
    retry_date = format_datetime(
        datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=30),
        usegmt=True,
    )
    dated_429 = worker._technical_failure_from_exception(
        DoubaoHTTPError(429, {"Retry-After": retry_date})
    )
    assert dated_429.retry_after_seconds is not None
    assert 0 < dated_429.retry_after_seconds <= 30

    first = retry_delay_seconds(1, jitter_key="job")
    second = retry_delay_seconds(2, jitter_key="job")
    assert second > first
    assert retry_delay_seconds(
        1, retry_after_seconds=9, jitter_key="job"
    ) == 9


def test_breaker_opens_in_short_window_and_requires_explicit_reset_state() -> None:
    now = datetime(2026, 7, 27, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    first = record_breaker_failure(now=now)
    second = record_breaker_failure(
        state=first.state,
        failure_count=first.failure_count,
        window_started_at=first.window_started_at,
        now=now,
    )
    third = record_breaker_failure(
        state=second.state,
        failure_count=second.failure_count,
        window_started_at=second.window_started_at,
        now=now,
    )
    assert third.state == "open"
    assert third.reason == "SHORT_WINDOW_FAILURE_THRESHOLD"
    assert third.cooldown_until is not None

    p0 = record_breaker_failure(now=now, retryable=False)
    assert p0.state == "open"
    assert p0.reason == "NON_RETRYABLE_P0"


def test_worker_technical_retry_uses_recovery_and_never_exceeds_two(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="retry.jpg",
        stored_name="retry.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="1" * 64,
    )
    bundle = StrategyBundle(
        canonical_hash="2" * 64,
        model_id="retry-model",
        model_config_snapshot="{}",
        prompt_a_version="retry-A",
        rubric_version="retry-rubric",
        engine_version="retry-engine",
    )
    db.add_all([asset, bundle])
    db.flush()
    loop_run = LoopRun(
        idempotency_key="retry-loop-key",
        request_fingerprint="3" * 64,
        asset_id=asset.id,
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
        status="waiting_result",
        current_round=2,
        decision_json="{}",
    )
    loop_attempt = LoopAttempt(
        business_round=2,
        kind="targeted_recheck",
        target_dimensions_json='["lighting"]',
        input_evidence_json="{}",
        status="waiting_result",
    )
    loop_run.attempts.append(loop_attempt)
    db.add(loop_run)
    db.flush()
    original = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        category_profile_snapshot_json='{"schema_version":"evaluation-category-profile-v1","category_key":"space_image"}',
        prompt_a_id=101,
        prompt_b_id=102,
        regression_item_id=201,
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
        status="processing",
        queue_class="production_batch",
        origin_queue_class="production_batch",
        batch_key="batch:retry",
        loop_attempt_id=loop_attempt.id,
    )
    db.add(original)
    db.commit()
    duplicate_root = EvaluationJob(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        prompt_a_id=101,
        prompt_b_id=102,
        regression_item_id=201,
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
        status="queued",
        queue_class="validation",
        origin_queue_class="validation",
        technical_attempt=0,
    )
    db.add(duplicate_root)
    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
        db.flush()
    db.rollback()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    try:
        assert worker._handle_technical_failure(
            original.id, TimeoutError("timed out")
        )
        jobs = db.scalars(
            select(EvaluationJob).order_by(EvaluationJob.id)
        ).all()
        assert [job.technical_attempt for job in jobs] == [0, 1]
        assert jobs[1].queue_class == "recovery"
        assert jobs[1].origin_queue_class == "production_batch"
        assert jobs[1].parent_job_id == original.id
        assert jobs[1].root_job_id == original.id
        assert jobs[1].loop_attempt_id == loop_attempt.id
        assert jobs[1].regression_item_id == 201
        assert jobs[1].prompt_a_id == 101
        assert jobs[1].prompt_b_id == 102
        assert (
            jobs[1].category_profile_snapshot_json
            == original.category_profile_snapshot_json
        )
        assert worker._handle_technical_failure(
            original.id, TimeoutError("duplicate callback")
        )
        assert (
            db.scalar(
                select(func.count(EvaluationJob.id)).where(
                    EvaluationJob.root_job_id == original.id
                )
            )
            == 2
        )
        jobs[1].status = "processing"
        db.commit()

        assert worker._handle_technical_failure(
            jobs[1].id, TimeoutError("timed out")
        )
        jobs = db.scalars(
            select(EvaluationJob).order_by(EvaluationJob.id)
        ).all()
        assert [job.technical_attempt for job in jobs] == [0, 1, 2]
        assert {job.root_job_id for job in jobs} == {original.id}
        assert {job.loop_attempt_id for job in jobs} == {loop_attempt.id}
        assert {job.regression_item_id for job in jobs} == {201}
        assert {job.prompt_a_id for job in jobs} == {101}
        assert {job.prompt_b_id for job in jobs} == {102}
        persisted_attempts = db.scalars(
            select(LoopAttempt).where(
                LoopAttempt.loop_run_id == loop_run.id
            )
        ).all()
        assert len(persisted_attempts) == 1
        assert persisted_attempts[0].business_round == 2
        jobs[2].status = "processing"
        db.commit()
        assert worker._handle_technical_failure(
            jobs[2].id, TimeoutError("timed out")
        ) is False
        assert (
            db.scalar(select(EvaluationJob).where(EvaluationJob.id > jobs[2].id))
            is None
        )
        breaker = db.scalar(
            select(CircuitBreaker).where(
                CircuitBreaker.scope_type == "batch",
                CircuitBreaker.scope_key == "batch:retry",
            )
        )
        assert breaker is not None
        assert breaker.state == "open"
        assert jobs[2].technical_attempt == 2
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_worker_non_retryable_failure_is_p0_without_recovery(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="p0.jpg",
        stored_name="p0.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="2" * 64,
    )
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        status="processing",
        batch_key="batch:p0",
    )
    db.add(job)
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    try:
        assert worker._handle_technical_failure(
            job.id, RuntimeError("invalid request")
        ) is False
        db.refresh(job)
        assert job.status == "failed"
        assert job.stage == "p0_error"
        # 保留可程序化解析的前缀，同时把真实原因原文带给运营。
        assert job.error_message == "technical:non_retryable\ninvalid request"
        assert db.scalar(select(EvaluationJob).where(EvaluationJob.parent_job_id == job.id)) is None
        breaker = db.scalar(
            select(CircuitBreaker).where(
                CircuitBreaker.scope_key == "batch:p0"
            )
        )
        assert breaker is not None
        assert breaker.state == "open"
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_retry_after_attack_values_never_leave_jobs_processing(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    values = ("inf", "nan", "1e309", "-1", "invalid", "999999")
    jobs = []
    for index, value in enumerate(values, start=1):
        asset = Asset(
            original_name=f"retry-after-{index}.jpg",
            stored_name=f"retry-after-{index}.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=10,
            sha256=(str(index) * 64)[:64],
        )
        job = EvaluationJob(
            asset=asset,
            status="processing",
            queue_class="production_batch",
            origin_queue_class="production_batch",
        )
        db.add(job)
        jobs.append((job, value))
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    started = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    try:
        for job, value in jobs:
            assert worker._handle_technical_failure(
                job.id,
                DoubaoHTTPError(429, {"Retry-After": value}),
            )
        all_jobs = db.scalars(
            select(EvaluationJob).order_by(EvaluationJob.id)
        ).all()
        roots = [job for job in all_jobs if job.technical_attempt == 0]
        children = {
            job.parent_job_id: job
            for job in all_jobs
            if job.technical_attempt == 1
        }
        assert len(roots) == len(values)
        assert len(children) == len(values)
        assert {job.status for job in roots} == {"retrying"}
        assert {job.status for job in children.values()} == {"queued"}
        for root, value in jobs:
            child = children[root.id]
            retry_at = child.retry_after_at
            assert retry_at is not None
            if retry_at.tzinfo is None:
                retry_at = retry_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            delay = (retry_at - started).total_seconds()
            if value == "999999":
                assert 3599 <= delay <= 3601
            else:
                assert 0 <= delay <= 5
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_concurrent_technical_failure_callbacks_create_one_child(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'retry-race.db').as_posix()}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False, "timeout": 10},
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    factory = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with factory() as db:
        asset = Asset(
            original_name="race.jpg",
            stored_name="race.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=10,
            sha256="8" * 64,
        )
        bundle = StrategyBundle(
            canonical_hash="9" * 64,
            model_id="race-model",
            model_config_snapshot="{}",
            prompt_a_version="race-A",
            rubric_version="race-rubric",
            engine_version="race-engine",
        )
        db.add_all([asset, bundle])
        db.flush()
        job = EvaluationJob(
            asset=asset,
            regression_item_id=901,
            strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
            status="processing",
            queue_class="production_batch",
            origin_queue_class="production_batch",
            batch_key="batch:race",
        )
        db.add(job)
        db.commit()
        job_id = job.id

    @contextmanager
    def concurrent_scope():
        db = factory()
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", concurrent_scope)
    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
            results = list(
                pool.map(
                    lambda _index: worker._handle_technical_failure(
                        job_id, TimeoutError("race")
                    ),
                    range(2),
                )
            )
        assert results == [True, True]
        with factory() as db:
            jobs = db.scalars(
                select(EvaluationJob).order_by(EvaluationJob.id)
            ).all()
            assert [job.technical_attempt for job in jobs] == [0, 1]
            assert jobs[1].root_job_id == jobs[0].id
            assert jobs[1].regression_item_id == 901
            assert jobs[1].strategy_bundle_id == bundle.id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_enqueue_compatibility_manual_canary_queue_status_and_breaker_api() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin",
        username="queue-tester",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="Queue Tester",
    )
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="queue.jpg",
        stored_name="queue.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="3" * 64,
    )
    prompt_a = PromptVersion(
        stage="A",
        name="A",
        version="queue-A",
        system_prompt="classification system prompt",
        user_prompt="classification input",
        status="published",
    )
    prompt_b = PromptVersion(
        stage="B",
        name="B",
        version="queue-B",
        system_prompt="aesthetic system prompt",
        user_prompt="aesthetic input",
        status="published",
    )
    model = ModelConfig(
        max_concurrency=20,
        encrypted_api_key="configured-test-reference",
    )
    dimension_definition = space_schema_definition_for_version(
        ACTIVE_V13_VERSION
    )
    dimension_schema = DimensionSchema(
        schema_key=SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY,
        version=ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
        schema_type="family_pack",
        family_key="space",
        display_name="空间现役维度",
        status="published",
        definition_json=canonical_json(dimension_definition),
        canonical_hash=canonical_hash(dimension_definition),
        created_by="test",
        published_by="test",
        published_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )
    db.add_all([user, asset, prompt_a, prompt_b, model, dimension_schema])
    add_active_v3_contract(db)
    db.commit()

    def test_db():
        yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = test_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        common = {
            "asset_ids": [asset.id],
            "prompt_a_id": prompt_a.id,
            "prompt_b_id": prompt_b.id,
        }
        default = client.post("/api/jobs/enqueue", json=common)
        manual = client.post(
            "/api/jobs/enqueue",
            json={**common, "manual_recheck": True},
        )
        canary = client.post(
            "/api/jobs/enqueue",
            json={**common, "queue_class": "canary"},
        )
        assert default.json()["queue_class"] == "production_batch"
        assert manual.json()["queue_class"] == "interactive"
        assert canary.json()["queue_class"] == "canary"
        rows = db.scalars(
            select(EvaluationJob).order_by(EvaluationJob.id)
        ).all()
        assert [row.queue_class for row in rows] == [
            "production_batch",
            "interactive",
            "canary",
        ]
        delayed = EvaluationJob(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            prompt_a_id=prompt_a.id,
            prompt_b_id=prompt_b.id,
            queue_class="production_batch",
            origin_queue_class="production_batch",
            retry_after_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            + timedelta(hours=1),
            status="queued",
        )
        db.add(delayed)
        db.commit()

        status = client.get("/api/queues/status")
        assert status.status_code == 200
        expected = {
            "production_batch": 50,
            "interactive": 20,
            "validation": 15,
            "canary": 10,
            "recovery": 5,
        }
        assert status.json()["shares"] == expected
        assert status.json()["weights"] == expected
        assert status.json()["validation_boost"] == 10
        assert status.json()["global_limit"] == 20
        queue_rows = {
            row["queue_class"]: row
            for row in status.json()["queues"]
        }
        assert queue_rows["interactive"]["pending"] == 1
        assert queue_rows["production_batch"]["pending"] == 2
        assert queue_rows["production_batch"]["pending_total"] == 2
        assert status.json()["credentials_configured"] is True
        assert status.json()["control_paused"] is False
        assert (
            queue_rows["production_batch"]["dispatchable_pending"] == 1
        )
        assert queue_rows["production_batch"]["blocked_by_credentials"] == 0
        assert queue_rows["production_batch"]["blocked_by_control"] == 0
        assert (
            queue_rows["production_batch"]["delayed_by_retry_after"] == 1
        )
        assert {
            queue: row["reserved"]
            for queue, row in queue_rows.items()
        } == {
            "production_batch": 10,
            "interactive": 4,
            "validation": 3,
            "canary": 2,
            "recovery": 1,
        }
        assert {
            queue: row["weight"]
            for queue, row in queue_rows.items()
        } == expected
        assert queue_rows["validation"]["effective_weight"] == 25
        assert queue_rows["production_batch"]["effective_weight"] == 50

        opened = client.post(
            "/api/circuit-breakers/batch/batch-api/open",
            json={"reason": "manual_test", "cooldown_seconds": 30},
        )
        assert opened.status_code == 200
        assert opened.json()["state"] == "open"
        reset = client.post(
            "/api/circuit-breakers/batch/batch-api/reset"
        )
        assert reset.status_code == 200
        assert reset.json()["state"] == "closed"
        assert reset.json()["reset_by"] == "queue-tester"
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_queue_status_matches_worker_credential_and_pause_gates(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin",
        username="queue-observer",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="Queue Observer",
    )
    model = ModelConfig(max_concurrency=2)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="blocked.jpg",
        stored_name="blocked.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="9" * 64,
    )
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        queue_class="interactive",
        origin_queue_class="interactive",
    )
    control = EvaluationControl(id=1, paused=False)
    db.add_all([user, model, job, control])
    db.commit()

    def test_db():
        yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = test_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        model.encrypted_api_key = "   "
        db.commit()
        blank = client.get("/api/queues/status").json()
        blank_row = next(
            item
            for item in blank["queues"]
            if item["queue_class"] == "interactive"
        )
        assert blank["credentials_configured"] is False
        assert blank["global_limit"] == 1
        assert blank_row["blocked_by_credentials"] == 1

        @contextmanager
        def test_scope():
            try:
                yield db
                db.commit()
            except Exception:
                db.rollback()
                raise

        monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
        assert worker.claim_next_job() is None

        model.encrypted_api_key = None
        db.commit()
        missing = client.get("/api/queues/status").json()
        missing_row = next(
            item
            for item in missing["queues"]
            if item["queue_class"] == "interactive"
        )
        assert missing["credentials_configured"] is False
        assert missing["global_limit"] == 1
        assert missing_row["pending_total"] == 1
        assert missing_row["blocked_by_credentials"] == 1
        assert missing_row["dispatchable_pending"] == 0

        model.encrypted_api_key = "configured-test-reference"
        control.paused = True
        db.commit()
        paused = client.get("/api/queues/status").json()
        paused_row = next(
            item
            for item in paused["queues"]
            if item["queue_class"] == "interactive"
        )
        assert paused["credentials_configured"] is True
        assert paused["control_paused"] is True
        assert paused_row["blocked_by_credentials"] == 0
        assert paused_row["blocked_by_control"] == 1
        assert paused_row["dispatchable_pending"] == 0
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_queue_status_and_worker_use_each_jobs_frozen_model_credentials(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin",
        username="frozen-credential-observer",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="Frozen Credential Observer",
    )
    fallback_model = ModelConfig(
        name="configured fallback",
        encrypted_api_key="configured-test-reference",
        max_concurrency=4,
        active=False,
    )
    missing_model = ModelConfig(
        name="frozen model without key",
        encrypted_api_key=None,
    )
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="frozen-model.jpg",
        stored_name="frozen-model.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="8" * 64,
    )
    db.add_all([user, fallback_model, missing_model, asset])
    db.flush()
    blocked = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        queue_class="interactive",
        origin_queue_class="interactive",
        category_profile_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "model_config_id": missing_model.id,
                "pdf_summary_model_config_id": None,
            }
        ),
    )
    available = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        queue_class="interactive",
        origin_queue_class="interactive",
        category_profile_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "model_config_id": fallback_model.id,
                "pdf_summary_model_config_id": None,
            }
        ),
    )
    db.add_all([blocked, available])
    db.commit()

    def test_db():
        yield db

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = test_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        status = client.get("/api/queues/status").json()
        row = next(
            item
            for item in status["queues"]
            if item["queue_class"] == "interactive"
        )
        assert status["credentials_configured"] is True
        assert row["pending_total"] == 2
        assert row["blocked_by_credentials"] == 1
        assert row["dispatchable_pending"] == 1

        assert worker.claim_next_job() == available.id
        db.refresh(blocked)
        db.refresh(available)
        assert blocked.status == "queued"
        assert available.status == "processing"
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_open_breaker_preserves_queued_job_and_claims_other_batch(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="breaker.jpg",
        stored_name="breaker.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="4" * 64,
    )
    model = ModelConfig(encrypted_api_key="not-used-by-claim")
    blocked = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        batch_key="batch:blocked",
        queue_class="production_batch",
    )
    available = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        batch_key="batch:available",
        queue_class="production_batch",
    )
    breaker = CircuitBreaker(
        scope_type="batch",
        scope_key="batch:blocked",
        state="open",
        failure_count=3,
    )
    db.add_all([model, blocked, available, breaker])
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    try:
        claimed = worker.claim_next_job()
        assert claimed == available.id
        db.refresh(blocked)
        db.refresh(available)
        assert blocked.status == "queued"
        assert available.status == "processing"
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_incremental_claim_aggregates_independent_provider_pool_capacity(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="independent-pools.jpg",
        stored_name="independent-pools.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="a" * 64,
    )
    first_pool = ModelQuotaPool(
        pool_key="doubao-endpoint-a",
        name="豆包端点 A",
        provider="doubao",
        scope_kind="endpoint",
        scope_identifier="ep-a",
        max_concurrency=1,
        lease_seconds=60,
        enabled=True,
        created_by="test",
    )
    second_pool = ModelQuotaPool(
        pool_key="doubao-endpoint-b",
        name="豆包端点 B",
        provider="doubao",
        scope_kind="endpoint",
        scope_identifier="ep-b",
        max_concurrency=1,
        lease_seconds=60,
        enabled=True,
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add_all([asset, first_pool, second_pool])
    db.flush()
    first_model = ModelConfig(
        name="端点 A 模型",
        encrypted_api_key="encrypted:test-a",
        max_concurrency=1,
        quota_pool_id=first_pool.id,
    )
    second_model = ModelConfig(
        name="端点 B 模型",
        encrypted_api_key="encrypted:test-b",
        max_concurrency=1,
        quota_pool_id=second_pool.id,
    )
    db.add_all([first_model, second_model])
    db.flush()
    first_job = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        queue_class="production_batch",
        origin_queue_class="production_batch",
        category_profile_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {"model_config_id": first_model.id}
        ),
    )
    second_job = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        queue_class="production_batch",
        origin_queue_class="production_batch",
        category_profile_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {"model_config_id": second_model.id}
        ),
    )
    db.add_all([first_job, second_job])
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    try:
        assert worker.claim_next_job() == first_job.id
        assert worker.claim_next_job() == second_job.id
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_incremental_claim_deduplicates_models_sharing_one_provider_pool(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="shared-pool.jpg",
        stored_name="shared-pool.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="b" * 64,
    )
    shared_pool = ModelQuotaPool(
        pool_key="doubao-shared-endpoint",
        name="豆包共享端点",
        provider="doubao",
        scope_kind="endpoint",
        scope_identifier="ep-shared",
        max_concurrency=1,
        lease_seconds=60,
        enabled=True,
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add_all([asset, shared_pool])
    db.flush()
    models = [
        ModelConfig(
            name=f"共享端点密钥 {index}",
            encrypted_api_key=f"encrypted:test-{index}",
            max_concurrency=10,
            quota_pool_id=shared_pool.id,
        )
        for index in (1, 2)
    ]
    db.add_all(models)
    db.flush()
    jobs = [
        EvaluationJob(
            asset=asset,
            queue_class="production_batch",
            origin_queue_class="production_batch",
            category_profile_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
                {"model_config_id": model.id}
            ),
        )
        for model in models
    ]
    db.add_all(jobs)
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    try:
        assert worker.claim_next_job() == jobs[0].id
        assert worker.claim_next_job() is None
        db.refresh(jobs[1])
        assert jobs[1].status == "queued"
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_saturated_pool_does_not_head_of_line_block_an_independent_pool(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="pool-head-of-line.jpg",
        stored_name="pool-head-of-line.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="c" * 64,
    )
    pools = [
        ModelQuotaPool(
            pool_key=f"doubao-hol-{suffix}",
            name=f"豆包端点 {suffix}",
            provider="doubao",
            scope_kind="endpoint",
            scope_identifier=f"ep-hol-{suffix}",
            max_concurrency=1,
            lease_seconds=60,
            enabled=True,
            created_by="test",
        )
        for suffix in ("a", "b")
    ]
    db.add_all([asset, *pools])
    db.flush()
    models = [
        ModelConfig(
            name=f"端点 {suffix} 模型",
            encrypted_api_key=f"encrypted:hol-{suffix}",
            max_concurrency=10,
            quota_pool_id=pool.id,
        )
        for suffix, pool in zip(("a", "b"), pools)
    ]
    db.add_all(models)
    db.flush()
    processing = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        status="processing",
        queue_class="production_batch",
        origin_queue_class="production_batch",
        category_profile_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {"model_config_id": models[0].id}
        ),
    )
    blocked = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        queue_class="production_batch",
        origin_queue_class="production_batch",
        category_profile_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {"model_config_id": models[0].id}
        ),
    )
    independent = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        queue_class="production_batch",
        origin_queue_class="production_batch",
        category_profile_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {"model_config_id": models[1].id}
        ),
    )
    db.add_all([processing, blocked, independent])
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    try:
        assert worker.claim_next_job() == independent.id
        db.refresh(blocked)
        assert blocked.status == "queued"
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_incremental_provider_call_holds_and_releases_pool_lease(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'incremental-quota.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(
            pool_key="doubao-incremental-call",
            name="豆包增量调用",
            provider="doubao",
            scope_kind="endpoint",
            scope_identifier="ep-incremental-call",
            max_concurrency=1,
            lease_seconds=60,
            enabled=True,
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        model = ModelConfig(
            name="增量模型",
            encrypted_api_key="encrypted:incremental",
            max_tokens=1_000,
            quota_pool_id=pool.id,
        )
        db.add(model)
        db.commit()
        model_id = model.id
        pool_id = pool.id

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        with sessions() as db:
            try:
                yield db
                db.commit()
            except Exception:
                db.rollback()
                raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)

    class FakeClient:
        async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            with sessions() as db:
                active = db.scalar(
                    select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(
                        ModelQuotaLease.pool_id == pool_id,
                        ModelQuotaLease.status == "active",
                    )
                )
                assert active == 1
            return "ok"

    managed = worker._QuotaManagedDoubaoClient(
        FakeClient(),
        job_id=17,
        technical_attempt=0,
        model_config_id=model_id,
        max_tokens=1_000,
    )
    assert asyncio.run(
        managed.chat_json("system", "user", image_path="image.jpg")
    ) == "ok"
    with sessions() as db:
        lease = db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease))
        assert lease is not None and lease.status == "released"
    engine.dispose()


def test_claim_large_queue_uses_bounded_candidate_select(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="large-queue.jpg",
        stored_name="large-queue.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="8" * 64,
    )
    model = ModelConfig(encrypted_api_key="not-used-by-claim", max_concurrency=10)
    db.add_all([asset, model])
    db.flush()
    db.bulk_save_objects([
        EvaluationJob(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            queue_class="production_batch",
            origin_queue_class="production_batch",
        )
        for _ in range(10_000)
    ])
    db.commit()

    queued_selects: list[str] = []

    def capture_statement(_conn, _cursor, statement, _parameters, _context, _many):
        normalized = statement.lower()
        if (
            normalized.lstrip().startswith("select")
            and "from evaluation_jobs" in normalized
            and "evaluation_jobs.status" in normalized
        ):
            queued_selects.append(normalized)

    event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", capture_statement)

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    try:
        assert worker.claim_next_job() is not None
        candidate_selects = [
            statement for statement in queued_selects
            if "order by evaluation_jobs.created_at" in statement
        ]
        assert candidate_selects
        assert all(" limit " in statement for statement in candidate_selects)
    finally:
        event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", capture_statement)
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("scope_type", "scope_key"),
    [
        ("batch", "batch:cooldown"),
        ("strategy", "1"),
    ],
)
def test_expired_breaker_resets_and_job_becomes_claimable(
    monkeypatch,
    scope_type: str,
    scope_key: str,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="expired-breaker.jpg",
        stored_name="expired-breaker.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="5" * 64,
    )
    model = ModelConfig(encrypted_api_key="not-used-by-claim")
    bundle = StrategyBundle(
        canonical_hash="6" * 64,
        model_id="expired-breaker-model",
        model_config_snapshot="{}",
        prompt_a_version="expired-breaker-A",
        rubric_version="expired-breaker-rubric",
        engine_version="expired-breaker-engine",
    )
    db.add_all([asset, model, bundle])
    db.flush()
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        batch_key="batch:cooldown",
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
        queue_class="production_batch",
    )
    breaker = CircuitBreaker(
        scope_type=scope_type,
        scope_key=(
            str(bundle.id) if scope_type == "strategy" else scope_key
        ),
        state="open",
        failure_count=3,
        window_started_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=2),
        last_failure_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=1),
        opened_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=1),
        cooldown_until=datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=1),
        reason="SHORT_WINDOW_FAILURE_THRESHOLD",
    )
    db.add_all([job, breaker])
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    try:
        assert worker.claim_next_job() == job.id
        db.refresh(breaker)
        assert breaker.state == "closed"
        assert breaker.failure_count == 0
        assert breaker.window_started_at is None
        assert breaker.opened_at is None
        assert breaker.cooldown_until is None
        assert breaker.reason is None
        assert breaker.reset_by == "system:cooldown"
        assert breaker.reset_at is not None
        assert breaker.last_failure_at is not None
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_future_breaker_cooldown_keeps_job_blocked(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="future-breaker.jpg",
        stored_name="future-breaker.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="7" * 64,
    )
    model = ModelConfig(encrypted_api_key="not-used-by-claim")
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset=asset,
        batch_key="batch:future-cooldown",
        queue_class="production_batch",
    )
    breaker = CircuitBreaker(
        scope_type="batch",
        scope_key="batch:future-cooldown",
        state="open",
        failure_count=3,
        cooldown_until=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=5),
        reason="SHORT_WINDOW_FAILURE_THRESHOLD",
    )
    db.add_all([model, job, breaker])
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    try:
        assert worker.claim_next_job() is None
        db.refresh(breaker)
        assert breaker.state == "open"
        assert breaker.reset_by is None
        assert job.status == "queued"
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_claim_lock_compiles_to_skip_locked_on_mysql_and_nothing_on_sqlite() -> None:
    """MySQL 抢任务先锁行再改（code review 2026-09-18）；SQLite 方言不生成 FOR UPDATE。"""
    from sqlalchemy import select
    from sqlalchemy.dialects import mysql, sqlite
    from app.models import EvaluationJob

    stmt = (
        select(EvaluationJob.id)
        .where(EvaluationJob.id == 1, EvaluationJob.status == "queued")
        .with_for_update(skip_locked=True)
    )
    assert "FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED" in str(stmt.compile(dialect=mysql.dialect()))
    assert "FOR UPDATE" not in str(stmt.compile(dialect=sqlite.dialect()))
    source = inspect.getsource(worker.claim_next_job)
    assert "with_for_update(skip_locked=True)" in source

===== FILE backend/tests/test_quota_headroom.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import timedelta

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app import model_quota
from app.models import ModelQuotaLease, ModelQuotaMinuteWindow, ModelQuotaPool
from test_model_quota import NOW, _pool


@pytest.fixture
def quota_db():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            yield db
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("capacity,batch_slots,online_slots", [(24, 22, 2), (2, 1, 1), (1, 1, 0)])
def test_batch_leaves_online_room_inside_provider_ceiling(quota_db, capacity, batch_slots, online_slots):
    pool = _pool(quota_db, key="shared", concurrency=capacity)
    headroom = model_quota.batch_concurrency_headroom(capacity)
    for index in range(batch_slots):
        model_quota.acquire_model_quota(
            quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key=f"batch:{index}",
            concurrency_headroom=headroom, now=NOW,
        )
    with pytest.raises(model_quota.ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
        model_quota.acquire_model_quota(
            quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="batch:overflow",
            concurrency_headroom=headroom, now=NOW,
        )
    assert exc.value.code in model_quota.LOCAL_QUOTA_WAIT_CODES
    assert exc.value.pool_key == "shared"
    assert exc.value.retry_after_seconds == 5
    for index in range(online_slots):
        model_quota.acquire_model_quota(
            quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key=f"online:{index}", now=NOW,
        )
    with pytest.raises(model_quota.ModelQuotaUnavailable):
        model_quota.acquire_model_quota(
            quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="online:overflow", now=NOW,
        )
    assert quota_db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == capacity
    assert pool.max_concurrency == capacity


def test_lower_cap_and_new_headroom_do_not_revoke_existing_lease(quota_db):
    pool = _pool(quota_db, key="lowered", concurrency=4)
    original = model_quota.acquire_model_quota(
        quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="batch:original", reserved_tokens=19, now=NOW,
    )
    model_quota.acquire_model_quota(
        quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="batch:other", now=NOW,
    )
    pool.max_concurrency = 1
    replay = model_quota.acquire_model_quota(
        quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="batch:original",
        reserved_tokens=19, concurrency_headroom=2, now=NOW,
    )
    assert replay.reused is True and replay.lease_token == original.lease_token
    assert quota_db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(ModelQuotaLease.status == "active")) == 2
    assert quota_db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaMinuteWindow.request_count)) == 2


@pytest.mark.parametrize("invalid", [-1, True, 1.0, "2", None])
def test_invalid_headroom_cannot_reserve_or_preflight(quota_db, invalid):
    pool = _pool(quota_db, key="invalid", concurrency=24)
    for operation in (model_quota.acquire_model_quota, model_quota.check_model_quota_available):
        kwargs = {"owner_key": "invalid:owner"} if operation is model_quota.acquire_model_quota else {}
        with pytest.raises(model_quota.ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
            operation(quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, concurrency_headroom=invalid, now=NOW, **kwargs)
        assert exc.value.code == "quota_concurrency_headroom_invalid"
    assert quota_db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 0
    assert quota_db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaMinuteWindow.id))) == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("invalid", [0, -1, True, 1.0, "24", None])
def test_invalid_pool_ceiling_fails_closed(invalid):
    with pytest.raises(model_quota.ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
        model_quota.batch_concurrency_headroom(invalid)
    assert exc.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_invalid"


def test_preflight_counts_only_unexpired_leases_and_never_writes(quota_db):
    pool = _pool(quota_db, key="preflight", concurrency=3)
    original = model_quota.acquire_model_quota(
        quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="old:request", now=NOW,
    )
    quota_db.commit()
    pool_id = pool.id
    pool.enabled = False  # An unrelated dirty ORM object must not be flushed by a read hint.
    statements = []
    def record_sql(connection, cursor, statement, parameters, context, executemany):
        statements.append(statement)
    event.listen(quota_db.get_bind(), "before_cursor_execute", record_sql)
    try:
        with pytest.raises(model_quota.ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
            model_quota.check_model_quota_available(
                quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, concurrency_headroom=2, now=NOW,
            )
        assert exc.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
        assert exc.value.pool_key == "preflight" and exc.value.retry_after_seconds == 5
        assert model_quota.check_model_quota_available(
            quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, concurrency_headroom=2,
            now=NOW + timedelta(seconds=30),
        ) is None
    finally:
        event.remove(quota_db.get_bind(), "before_cursor_execute", record_sql)
    assert all(statement.lstrip().upper().startswith("SELECT") for statement in statements)
    quota_db.rollback()
    lease = quota_db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease).where(ModelQuotaLease.lease_token == original.lease_token))
    assert lease.status == "active" and lease.released_at is None
    assert quota_db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaMinuteWindow.request_count)) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("condition,code,retry", [
    ("disabled", "quota_pool_disabled", None),
    ("cooldown", "quota_pool_cooling_down", 20),
    ("missing", "quota_pool_missing", None),
])
def test_preflight_checks_current_pool_state(quota_db, condition, code, retry):
    pool = _pool(quota_db, key="state", concurrency=3)
    quota_db.commit()
    pool_id = pool.id
    # Bypass the identity map to reproduce a pool changed by another worker.
    values = {"enabled": False} if condition == "disabled" else {"cooldown_until": NOW + timedelta(seconds=20)}
    if condition != "missing":
        quota_db.execute(update(ModelQuotaPool).where(ModelQuotaPool.id == pool_id).values(**values).execution_options(synchronize_session=False))
    with pytest.raises(model_quota.ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
        model_quota.check_model_quota_available(quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool_id if condition != "missing" else 999, now=NOW)
    assert exc.value.code == code and exc.value.retry_after_seconds == retry
    if condition != "missing":
        assert exc.value.pool_key == "state"
    assert quota_db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 0
    assert quota_db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaMinuteWindow.id))) == 0


def test_successful_preflight_does_not_reserve_or_override_authoritative_check(quota_db):
    pool = _pool(quota_db, key="race", concurrency=1)
    quota_db.commit()
    model_quota.check_model_quota_available(quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, now=NOW)
    assert quota_db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 0
    model_quota.acquire_model_quota(quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="other:wins", now=NOW)
    with pytest.raises(model_quota.ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
        model_quota.acquire_model_quota(quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="preflight:loses", now=NOW)
    assert exc.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"


def _mixed_reservations(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db:
        pool = _pool(db, key="mixed", concurrency=24)
        pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()
    def reserve(item):
        index, headroom = item
        with Session(engine) as db:
            try:
                reservation = model_quota.acquire_model_quota(
                    db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key=f"request:{index}",
                    concurrency_headroom=headroom, now=NOW,
                )
                db.commit()
                return headroom, reservation.lease_token
            except model_quota.ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
                db.rollback()
                assert exc.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
                return headroom, None
    requests = [(index, 0 if index in {5, 17} else 2) for index in range(48)]
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=12) as workers:
        outcomes = list(workers.map(reserve, requests))
    assert sum(token is not None for headroom, token in outcomes if headroom == 0) == 2
    assert sum(token is not None for headroom, token in outcomes if headroom == 2) <= 22
    with Session(engine) as db:
        active = db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(ModelQuotaLease.status == "active"))
        assert active <= 24
        assert active == len({token for _, token in outcomes if token is not None})


def test_mixed_sqlite_workers_keep_online_room_and_total_ceiling(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'mixed-quota.db'}", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False, "timeout": 30})
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        _mixed_reservations(engine)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mixed_mysql_workers_keep_online_room_and_total_ceiling(mysql_engine):
    from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import mysql_metadata
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    for model in (ModelQuotaPool, ModelQuotaLease, ModelQuotaMinuteWindow):
        metadata.tables[model.__tablename__].create(mysql_engine)
    _mixed_reservations(mysql_engine)


def test_authoritative_admission_refreshes_a_cached_lowered_pool(quota_db):
    pool = _pool(quota_db, key="cached-cap", concurrency=4)
    model_quota.acquire_model_quota(quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="first:request", now=NOW)
    quota_db.execute(update(ModelQuotaPool).where(ModelQuotaPool.id == pool.id).values(max_concurrency=1).execution_options(synchronize_session=False))
    assert pool.max_concurrency == 4
    with pytest.raises(model_quota.ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
        model_quota.acquire_model_quota(quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="cached:overflow", now=NOW)
    assert exc.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"


def _workflow_quota_case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import json
    from app.audit import canonical_json
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case

    run, attempt, node, model, _ = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    pool = _pool(db, key="workflow-shared", concurrency=3)
    model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
    snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
    runtime = snapshot["runtime_context"]
    limits = runtime["real_execution_node_limits"][node["node_instance_id"]]
    limits["online_fallback_limits"] = dict(limits)
    runtime["inference_route"] = {
        "mode": "batch", "endpoint_id": "ep-bi-headroom", "foundation_model": model.model_id,
        "fallback_policy": {
            "version": "batch-online-fallback-v1", "batch_max_attempts": 3,
            "deadline_seconds": 86400,
            "online_route": {"mode": "online", "endpoint_id": "ep-headroom", "foundation_model": model.model_id},
        },
    }
    run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
    db.commit()
    return pool, {"attempt_id": attempt.id, "node": node, "run_snapshot": snapshot}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("transport,endpoint,batch", [
    ("batch", "ep-bi-headroom", True),
    (None, "ep-bi-headroom", True),
    (None, None, True),
    (None, "ep-headroom", False),
    ("online", "ep-headroom", False),
])
def test_workflow_reservations_apply_headroom_to_actual_batch_route(quota_db, tmp_path, monkeypatch, transport, endpoint, batch):
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation, WorkflowModelBudgetError

    pool, kwargs = _workflow_quota_case(quota_db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    model_quota.acquire_model_quota(quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="occupied:request")
    kwargs.update(transport_mode=transport, endpoint_id=endpoint)
    if batch:
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc:
            reserve_model_invocation(quota_db, **kwargs)
        assert exc.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
        assert exc.value.pool_key == "workflow-shared" and exc.value.retry_after_seconds == 5
        assert quota_db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 0
    else:
        invocation = reserve_model_invocation(quota_db, **kwargs)
        assert invocation.status == "reserved"
        assert quota_db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 2


@pytest.mark.parametrize("transport", [None, "batch"])
def test_workflow_reuses_live_batch_reservation_but_reacquires_expired_one_with_headroom(quota_db, tmp_path, monkeypatch, transport):
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation, WorkflowModelBudgetError

    pool, kwargs = _workflow_quota_case(quota_db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    kwargs.update(transport_mode=transport, endpoint_id="ep-bi-headroom")
    invocation = reserve_model_invocation(quota_db, **kwargs)
    first_token = invocation.quota_lease_token
    model_quota.acquire_model_quota(quota_db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="online:request")
    replay = reserve_model_invocation(quota_db, **kwargs)
    assert replay.id == invocation.id and replay.quota_lease_token == first_token
    lease = quota_db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease).where(ModelQuotaLease.lease_token == first_token))
    lease.expires_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=1)
    quota_db.flush()
    with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc:
        reserve_model_invocation(quota_db, **kwargs)
    assert exc.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
    assert quota_db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaMinuteWindow.request_count)) == 2

===== FILE backend/tests/test_qwen_205_vision_profile.py =====
from copy import deepcopy
import pytest
from app.vision_input_policy import (QWEN_205_PROFILE, SEED_20_PROFILE, DetailFidelity, OriginalPreprocess,
    VisionRolePolicy, VisionInputPolicyError, estimate_image_tokens, parse_vision_capability_profile)


def capability(raw=QWEN_205_PROFILE, provider='local_qwen'):
    return parse_vision_capability_profile(raw,provider=provider,model_id='qwen3.8-27b')


# Frozen deployed Transformers smart_resize results. The final four also
# match /tokenize count minus the identical 18 text/control tokens.
@pytest.mark.parametrize('width,height,tokens',[(1024,1024,1024),(1000,750,713),
    (10000,5000,990),(8000,6000,972),(15,15,49),
    (5539,7962,988),(5792,8688,1014),(8192,5464,1014),(7362,5779,1008)])
def test_qwen_deployed_patch_and_merge_formula(width,height,tokens):
    role=VisionRolePolicy(fidelity=DetailFidelity('high'),preprocess=OriginalPreprocess())
    assert estimate_image_tokens(capability(),role,width,height)==tokens


def test_large_original_is_admitted_without_changing_seed_limit():
    c=capability()
    c.validate_media(mime_type='image/jpeg',width=10000,height=5000)
    assert c.operational_single_image_bytes > 15_000_000
    with pytest.raises(VisionInputPolicyError):
        capability(SEED_20_PROFILE,'doubao').validate_media(mime_type='image/jpeg',width=10000,height=5000)
    with pytest.raises(VisionInputPolicyError):
        c.validate_media(mime_type='image/jpeg',width=10000,height=7000)


def test_deployment_formula_cannot_be_used_with_drifting_parameters():
    for field,value in [('max_source_pixels',80_000_000),('detail_modes',['high','xhigh']),
                         ('max_single_image_bytes',33_000_000),('profile_key','fake')]:
        raw=deepcopy(dict(QWEN_205_PROFILE));raw[field]=value
        with pytest.raises(VisionInputPolicyError): capability(raw)
    with pytest.raises(VisionInputPolicyError): capability(provider='doubao')


def test_qwen_rejects_unsupported_detail_and_keeps_provider_actual_separate():
    c=capability()
    with pytest.raises(VisionInputPolicyError):
        estimate_image_tokens(c,VisionRolePolicy(fidelity=DetailFidelity('xhigh'),preprocess=OriginalPreprocess()),1024,1024)
    assert c.token_formula != SEED_20_PROFILE['token_formula']
    assert c.max_image_tokens(DetailFidelity('high'))==1024


def test_qwen_default_policy_can_be_authored_and_frozen_with_the_real_model(tmp_path):
    import json
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    from app.models import ModelRegistryEntry, ModelConfig
    from app.vision_input_policy import default_vision_input_policy
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_authored_vision_input_policy, freeze_component_references, validate_managed_workflow_v3
    from test_workflow_working_copies import _engine, _seed_managed_dependencies
    policy=default_vision_input_policy(capability()).as_dict()
    assert validate_authored_vision_input_policy(policy).as_dict()==policy
    engine=_engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _,manifest=_seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            prompt=next(n for n in manifest['nodes'] if n['kind']=='prompt')
            registry=db.get(ModelRegistryEntry,prompt['config']['model_registry_id'])
            source=db.get(ModelConfig,registry.source_model_config_id)
            for model in (registry,source):
                model.provider='local_qwen';model.protocol='openai_chat';model.model_id='qwen3.8-27b'
                model.vision_capability_profile_json=json.dumps(dict(QWEN_205_PROFILE))
            prompt['config']['vision_input_policy']=policy
            before=deepcopy(manifest)
            frozen=freeze_component_references(db,manifest)
            assert manifest==before
            assert frozen['nodes'][1]['config']['vision_capability_snapshot']['token_formula']==QWEN_205_PROFILE['token_formula']
            assert validate_managed_workflow_v3(db,frozen)==[]
            registry.provider='doubao'
            assert any('vision' in e.code for e in validate_managed_workflow_v3(db,manifest))
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_qwen_full_run_audit.py =====
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path


def test_unscored_items_stay_in_frozen_truth_recall_denominator():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'scripts/audit_qwen_full_run.py'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('qwen_audit', path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    rows = [{'truth': 'L1', 'prediction': 'L2'},
            {'truth': 'L2', 'prediction': None},
            {'truth': 'L3', 'prediction': 'L1'},
            {'truth': 'L5', 'prediction': 'L5'}]
    assert module.metrics(rows, {'L1', 'L2'}) == {
        'tp': 1, 'truth': 2, 'predicted': 2, 'precision': .5, 'recall': .5}
    assert module.metrics(rows, {'L5'}) == {
        'tp': 1, 'truth': 1, 'predicted': 1, 'precision': 1., 'recall': 1.}
    assert module.metrics([], {'L1', 'L2'}) == {
        'tp': 0, 'truth': 0, 'predicted': 0, 'precision': None, 'recall': None}

===== FILE backend/tests/test_r7_maintenance_pacing.py =====
"""Explicit audited maintenance is the only new idle exemption."""
import json
from datetime import timedelta
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.audit import append_audit_event
from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation, ModelQuotaPool
from app.model_quota import ModelQuotaUnavailable
from test_batch_submission_pacing import database, admit, NOW


def warm(db, pool):
    admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
    row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
    evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
    evidence['reservation']['batch_submission'].update(requests_per_minute=50,
        tokens_per_minute=1500000)
    row.evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence)
    db.commit()


def maintenance(db, pool, incident, start, end):
    from app.batch_pacing_maintenance import record_pool_maintenance
    for paused, second in [(True, start), (False, end)]:
        record_pool_maintenance(db, pool_id=pool, incident=incident, paused=paused,
            actor='test', now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=second))
    db.commit()


def test_half_hour_maintenance_preserves_rate_without_ramp_credit_or_burst(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        warm(db, pool)
        maintenance(db, pool, 'release', 10, 1810)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=1820))
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == 50
        assert state['tokens_per_minute'] == 1500000
        assert state['maintenance_idle_seconds'] == 1800
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
            admit(db, pool, 3, NOW+timedelta(seconds=1820))


def test_short_maintenance_does_not_purchase_ramp_progress(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        warm(db, pool)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        evidence['reservation']['batch_submission']['ramp_started_at'] = NOW.timestamp()-100
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence); db.commit()
        maintenance(db, pool, 'short', 10, 110)
        # 100 previous effective seconds + 20 new non-maintenance seconds < 180.
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=120))
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == 50
        assert state['maintenance_idle_seconds'] == 100


@pytest.mark.parametrize('seconds,maintained', [(600, False), (90000, True)])
def test_ordinary_idle_and_over_24h_always_restart(database, seconds, maintained):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        warm(db, pool)
        if maintained:
            maintenance(db, pool, 'long', 10, seconds-10)
        assert admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=seconds))['requests_per_minute'] <= 2


def test_overlapping_maintenance_is_union_and_never_hides_retry_after(database):
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        warm(db, pool)
        maintenance(db, pool, 'a', 10, 1010)
        maintenance(db, pool, 'b', 500, 1810)
        row = WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=99, step_attempt_id=99,
            invocation_key='older', node_instance_id='prompt', provider='doubao',
            model_id='test', endpoint_id='ep-bi-test', quota_pool_id=pool,
            status='failed', upstream_status_code=429, created_at=NOW-timedelta(seconds=300),
            finished_at=NOW+timedelta(seconds=20), evidence_json=json.dumps({'failure': {
                'provider_error_code':'ServerOverloaded', 'retry_after_seconds':7200}}))
        db.add(row); db.commit()
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
            check_submission(db, pool=db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool), reserved_tokens=30000,
                now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1820))
        assert caught.value.retry_after_seconds == 5400
        from app.batch_pacing_maintenance import maintenance_intervals
        assert maintenance_intervals(db, pool_id=pool, start=NOW.timestamp(),
            now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1820)) == [(NOW.timestamp()+10, NOW.timestamp()+1810)]


def test_controller_change_cold_starts_even_with_audited_maintenance(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        warm(db, pool)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))

        assert admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=1820))['requests_per_minute'] == 2


def test_unmatched_open_pause_does_not_exempt_idle(database):
    from app.batch_pacing_maintenance import record_pool_maintenance
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        warm(db, pool)

            now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=10))
