def test_resource_backoff_and_maintenance_overlap_is_not_double_subtracted(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        # Physical submission is older; its late completion overlaps maintenance.
        db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=99, step_attempt_id=99,
            invocation_key='older', node_instance_id='prompt', provider='doubao',
            model_id='test', endpoint_id='ep-bi-test', quota_pool_id=pool,
            status='failed', upstream_status_code=429, created_at=NOW-timedelta(seconds=300),
            finished_at=NOW+timedelta(seconds=10), evidence_json=json.dumps({'failure': {
                'provider_error_code':'ServerOverloaded', 'retry_after_seconds':600}})))
        db.flush()
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()))
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        evidence['reservation']['batch_submission'].update(requests_per_minute=50, tokens_per_minute=1500000)
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence); db.commit()
        maintenance(db, pool, 'overlap', 10, 610)
        # 1000 - union(600, 600) == 400 seconds ordinary idle, so cold.
        assert admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=1000))['requests_per_minute'] == 2


def test_resume_committed_while_candidate_waits_for_pool_lock_is_visible(database, monkeypatch):
    import threading
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    from app import model_quota
    from app.batch_pacing_maintenance import record_pool_maintenance
    engine, pool = database
    base = datetime.now(timezone.utc)-timedelta(seconds=1820)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, base)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        evidence['reservation']['batch_submission'].update(requests_per_minute=50, tokens_per_minute=1500000)
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence); db.commit()
        record_pool_maintenance(db, pool_id=pool, incident='race', paused=True,
            actor='test', now=base+timedelta(seconds=10))
        db.commit()
    waiting, resumed = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
    original = model_quota.reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction
    def delayed(db):
        if threading.current_thread().name == 'candidate':
            waiting.set()
            assert resumed.wait(5)
        return original(db)
    monkeypatch.setattr(model_quota, 'reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction', delayed)
    states, errors = [], []
    def candidate():
        try:
            with Session(engine) as db:
                states.append(model_quota.acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool,
                    owner_key='race', reserved_tokens=30000, batch_submission=True).submission_state)
                db.rollback()
        except BaseException as exc:
            errors.append(exc)
    thread = threading.Thread(target=candidate, name='candidate')
    thread.start()
    try:
        assert waiting.wait(5)
        with Session(engine) as db:
            record_pool_maintenance(db, pool_id=pool, incident='race', paused=False, actor='test')
            db.commit()
    finally:
        resumed.set()
        thread.join(5)
    assert not thread.is_alive() and not errors
    assert states[0]['requests_per_minute'] == 50
    assert states[0]['maintenance_idle_seconds'] >= 1810

===== FILE backend/tests/test_r7_observability.py =====
"""Observability must attribute threads and include the transaction lock tail."""
import asyncio
import time
import pytest
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime
from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota
from test_batch_submission_pacing import database


def test_runtime_records_quota_wait_and_transaction_hold_on_commit_and_rollback(database):
    engine, pool = database
    async def run():
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=1) as runtime:
            def transact(commit):
                with Session(engine) as db:
                    acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool, owner_key=f'obs:{commit}')
                    time.sleep(.01)  # The lock remains held AFTER acquire returns.
                    db.commit() if commit else db.rollback()
            await runtime.phase(transact, True)
            await runtime.phase(transact, False)
            result = runtime.lock_metrics.snapshot()
            assert result['pool_lock_wait']['count'] == 2
            assert result['pool_lock_hold']['count'] == 2
            assert result['pool_lock_hold']['p50_seconds'] >= .01
            assert result['pool_lock_hold']['peak_seconds'] >= .01
    asyncio.run(run())


def test_cpu_observer_reports_host_and_process_separately_and_tolerates_missing_proc(monkeypatch):
    from pathlib import Path
    from app.runtime_observability import ProcessResourceSampler
    ticks = [0]
    def read(path, *args, **kwargs):
        if str(path) == '/proc/stat':
            return ['cpu 100 0 100 800 0 0 0 0\n', 'cpu 140 0 110 850 0 0 0 0\n'][ticks[0]]
        if str(path) == '/proc/self/stat':
            fields = ['0'] * 22
            fields[0] = 'R'
            fields[11], fields[12], fields[21] = str(10+20*ticks[0]), '0', '256'
            return '123 (worker name (test)) ' + ' '.join(fields)
        raise FileNotFoundError
    monkeypatch.setattr(Path, 'read_text', read)
    monkeypatch.setattr(time, 'monotonic', lambda: 10+ticks[0]*2)
    import os
    monkeypatch.setattr(os, 'sysconf', lambda key: 100 if key == 'SC_CLK_TCK' else 4096)
    sampler = ProcessResourceSampler()
    assert sampler.collect()['process_cpu_percent'] is None
    ticks[0] = 1
    sample = sampler.collect()
    assert sample['host_busy_percent'] == 50
    assert sample['process_cpu_percent'] == 10
    assert sample['process_rss_bytes'] == 1048576
    monkeypatch.setattr(Path, 'read_text', lambda *a, **k: (_ for _ in ()).throw(FileNotFoundError()))
    assert sampler.collect()['host_busy_percent'] is None


def test_async_worker_persists_observations_even_without_autotune(database, monkeypatch):
    import json
    from sqlalchemy import select
    from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
    from app import async_runtime_worker, database as db_module, workflow_fixture_executor
    from app.models import AutomationWorkerStatus
    engine, _ = database
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine)
    monkeypatch.setattr(db_module, 'SessionLocal', sessions)
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_AUTOTUNE', '0')
    running = [True]
    async def step(*args, **kwargs):
        running[0] = False
        return True
    monkeypatch.setattr(workflow_fixture_executor, 'process_runtime_step_async', step)
    asyncio.run(async_runtime_worker.run_async_worker(slots=1, should_continue=lambda: running[0]))
    with sessions() as db:
        payload = json.loads(db.scalar(select(AutomationWorkerStatus)).last_result_json)
        assert payload['runtime'] == 'bounded_async'
        assert {'host_busy_percent', 'process_cpu_percent', 'process_rss_bytes',
            'phase_peak_seconds', 'phase_queue_peak_seconds', 'pool_lock_wait_peak_seconds',
            'pool_lock_hold_peak_seconds', 'pool_lock_hold', 'resource_sampled_at'} <= payload.keys()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_r7_provider_requeue.py =====
"""Drive the real generator: no provider access or real recovery sleeps."""
import asyncio
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
from app.doubao import DoubaoTransportError
from app.models import ModelConfig, ModelQuotaPool, ModelQuotaLease, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem, WorkflowModelInvocation
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case
from test_batch_failure_breaker import shared_case


@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind,delay', [('timeout', 60), ('network', 300)])
def test_provider_unknown_leaves_generator_and_parks_without_step_attempt(fallback_case, kind, delay):
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='r7', name='r7', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='r7', max_concurrency=24,
            lease_seconds=900, created_by='test')
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()

    class Client:
        def __init__(self, config): pass
        async def chat_json(self, *args, **options):
            raise DoubaoTransportError(kind)

    operation = executor._real_prompt_steps(**kwargs, model_client_type=Client)
    wait = next(operation)
    assert wait.heartbeat is not None
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoTransportError) as provider:
        asyncio.run(wait.call())
    try:
        with pytest.raises(executor.FixtureExecutionError) as parked:
            operation.throw(provider.value)
    finally:
        operation.close()
    assert parked.value.code == 'model_provider_resource_wait'
    assert parked.value.retry_after_seconds == delay
    before = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    executor._report_runtime_step_failure_with_retry(attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'],
        lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'], exc=parked.value)
    with sessions() as db:
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        assert row.status == 'failed' and row.error_code == 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown'
        assert row.estimated_cost_micros > 0
        assert json.loads(row.evidence_json)['failure']['technical_error_type'] == kind
        assert db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease)).status == 'released'
        attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id'])
        assert attempt.status == 'retryable' and attempt.attempt_no == 1
        assert attempt.lease_token is None
        dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id))
        assert dispatch.status == 'queued' and dispatch.lease_token is None
        assert dispatch.available_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) >= before + timedelta(seconds=delay)


def test_batch_only_cap_reclaims_after_60s_with_new_physical_identity(shared_case, monkeypatch):
    from app.batch_experiment import batch_only_experiment
    from app.models import ProductionRun
    from app.workflow_runtime import claim_next_runtime_step
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from test_batch_experiment import start
    from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import _drive_without_cooldown
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    kwargs = dict(cases[0])
    run_id = start(sessions, kwargs)

    class Client:
        fail = True
        def __init__(self, config): self.config = config
        async def chat_json(self, *args, **options):
            assert self.config.endpoint_id.startswith('ep-bi-')
            if self.fail:
                raise DoubaoTransportError('timeout')
            return DoubaoResponse(parsed={'score':88, 'label':'通过'}, raw_text='{}', raw_payload={},
                upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id='r7-offline', attempt_count=1,
                output_budget=options['output_budget'], reasoning_effort=options['reasoning_effort'],
                thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode, input_tokens=80, output_tokens=20, total_tokens=100)
    with pytest.raises(executor.FixtureExecutionError) as parked:
        _drive_without_cooldown(executor._real_prompt_steps(**kwargs, model_client_type=Client))
    executor._report_runtime_step_failure_with_retry(attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'],
        lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'], exc=parked.value)
    with sessions() as db:
        old = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        old_facts = (old.id, old.invocation_key, old.estimated_cost_micros, old.evidence_json)
        dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == kwargs['attempt_id']))
        at = dispatch.available_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        assert claim_next_runtime_step(db, 'worker-1', global_limit=100, now=at-timedelta(microseconds=1)) is None
        db.rollback()
    with sessions() as db:
        claimed = claim_next_runtime_step(db, 'worker-1', global_limit=100, now=at)
        assert claimed == kwargs['attempt_id']
        attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, claimed)
        kwargs['lease_token'] = attempt.lease_token
        assert attempt.attempt_no == 1 and batch_only_experiment(db, db.get(ProductionRun, run_id))
        db.commit()
    class Clock(datetime):
        @classmethod
        def now(cls, tz=None): return at if tz else at.replace(tzinfo=None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, 'datetime', Clock)
    Client.fail = False
    result = _drive_without_cooldown(executor._real_prompt_steps(**kwargs, model_client_type=Client))
    assert result.output_manifest['output']['score'] == 88
    with sessions() as db:
        rows = db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)).all()
        assert len(rows) == 2
        assert (rows[0].id, rows[0].invocation_key, rows[0].estimated_cost_micros, rows[0].evidence_json) == old_facts
        assert rows[1].id != rows[0].id and rows[1].invocation_key.endswith('batch:1:reservation:2')
        assert db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id']).attempt_no == 1

===== FILE backend/tests/test_ramp_slot_feedback.py =====
"""The ramp stops climbing once the local slots, not the provider, are the limit.

Measured on 2026-09-15 after the ramp fix: the rate reached 21.4 RPM while the
pool holds 96 concurrent calls that each last 100-420 seconds. Above roughly
max_concurrency / average duration the slots bind, so every further ×1.2 step
buys queue depth and nothing else - and leaves a high rate primed to fire the
moment a block of slots frees, which is precisely the burst the vendor's
RequestBurstTooFast protects against.

The gate only ever withholds a climb. It must never cut the rate: the provider
is refusing nothing here, we are out of local capacity, and a cut would only
slow the recovery once slots free.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import timedelta

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.batch_submission import SLOT_UTILISATION_CEILING, check_submission
from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota
from app.models import ModelQuotaPool

from test_batch_submission_pacing import database, admit, NOW

# The fixture pool holds 24; the hard concurrency check refuses at 24 itself.
CEILING_AT = 24


def _hold(db, pool_id, count, *, now=NOW):
    """Leave `count` leases active, the way an in-flight provider call does."""
    for index in range(count):
        acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key=f'held:{index}',
                            reserved_tokens=30000, now=now)
    db.flush()


def _ramped(db, pool_id, *, number, seconds):
    """One admission a full ramp window later; returns its pacing state.

    The intermediate admission is required, not decoration: idle_threshold is
    180 s here, so a single 200 s jump would trip the cold-idle reset and the
    rate would fall back to START_RPM, masking whatever the ramp did. Held
    leases alone do not refresh it - only an admission does.
    """
    admit(db, pool_id, 100 + number, NOW + timedelta(seconds=seconds/2))
    return admit(db, pool_id, number, NOW + timedelta(seconds=seconds))


def test_the_ramp_climbs_while_slots_are_slack(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        first = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        _hold(db, pool, 4)  # 4/24 = 17%
        later = _ramped(db, pool, number=2, seconds=200)
        assert later['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(first['requests_per_minute']*1.2)
        assert later['ramp_held_by_slots'] is False
        assert later['slot_utilisation'] == pytest.approx(4/CEILING_AT, abs=1e-4)


def test_the_ramp_is_held_once_slots_are_saturated(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        first = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        _hold(db, pool, 21)  # 21/24 = 87.5%, above the ceiling
        later = _ramped(db, pool, number=2, seconds=200)
        assert later['ramp_held_by_slots'] is True
        # Held, never cut.
        assert later['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(first['requests_per_minute'])
        assert later['tokens_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(first['tokens_per_minute'])


# 20/24 = 83% is under the ceiling, 21/24 = 87.5% is over it.
@pytest.mark.parametrize('held,expect_hold', [
    (int(SLOT_UTILISATION_CEILING * CEILING_AT), False),
    (int(SLOT_UTILISATION_CEILING * CEILING_AT) + 1, True),
])
def test_the_ceiling_is_where_the_behaviour_changes(database, held, expect_hold):
    # A fresh database per case: reusing one fixture across both would run the
    # second against the leases and evidence the first left behind.
    assert (held - 1)/CEILING_AT < SLOT_UTILISATION_CEILING
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        first = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        _hold(db, pool, held)
        later = _ramped(db, pool, number=2, seconds=200)
        assert later['ramp_held_by_slots'] is expect_hold, held
        climbed = later['requests_per_minute'] > first['requests_per_minute']
        assert climbed is (not expect_hold), held


def test_the_rate_resumes_climbing_when_slots_free(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        first = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        _hold(db, pool, 21)
        held = _ramped(db, pool, number=2, seconds=200)
        assert held['ramp_held_by_slots'] is True
        assert held['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(first['requests_per_minute'])
    # Slots free: the very next admission takes the step it was owed, once.
    with Session(engine) as db:
        from app.models import ModelQuotaLease
        for lease in db.scalars(select(ModelQuotaLease).where(ModelQuotaLease.status == 'active')):
            lease.status = 'released'
        db.commit()
        resumed = admit(db, pool, 3, NOW + timedelta(seconds=260))
        assert resumed['ramp_held_by_slots'] is False
        assert resumed['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(first['requests_per_minute']*1.2)


def test_saturation_never_reduces_the_rate_the_way_pressure_does(database):
    """Two different situations, two different answers."""
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        first = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        _hold(db, pool, 23)  # 96% - as saturated as the pool can be
        later = _ramped(db, pool, number=2, seconds=400)
        assert later['requests_per_minute'] >= first['requests_per_minute']
        assert later['feedback']['pressure'] is False


def test_an_unknown_slot_count_never_holds_the_ramp(database):
    # check_submission is also reachable without the caller's count; absent
    # evidence must not be read as saturation.
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        state = check_submission(db, pool=db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool), reserved_tokens=30000,
                                 now=NOW + timedelta(seconds=200), active_leases=None)
        assert state['slot_utilisation'] is None and state['ramp_held_by_slots'] is False


def test_the_state_is_recorded_so_a_held_ramp_is_visible(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        _hold(db, pool, 21)
        state = _ramped(db, pool, number=2, seconds=200)
        assert set(('slot_utilisation', 'ramp_held_by_slots')) <= set(state)
        # It survives the round trip into invocation evidence, which is where
        # the pacing state is actually persisted.
        from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()))
        stored = json.loads(row.evidence_json)['reservation']['batch_submission']
        assert stored['ramp_held_by_slots'] is True
        assert stored['slot_utilisation'] == pytest.approx(21/CEILING_AT, abs=1e-4)

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import json

import pytest

from app.api_datetime_encoding import platform_isoformat


def scope():
    return {'revision': 'fixture', 'workers': ['worker-1'],
            'workloads': [{'category_key': 'space_image', 'workflow_definition_id': 1}]}


def sample():
    return dict(workers_seen=1, workers_expected=1, submitted_total=1, settled_total=1,
                scope_revision='fixture',
                backlog_high=0, backlog_normal=1, oldest_normal_age_seconds=1,
                dispatch_paused=False, held=0, reserved_over_420=0,
                responsibility_micros=0, returned_429=0, returned_total=1)


def test_journal_resumes_logs_real_four_hours_and_detects_missing_samples(tmp_path):
    from app.readiness_monitor import Journal, Monitor
    start = datetime(2026, 9, 14, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    path = tmp_path / 'observations.jsonl'
    with Journal(path, scope()) as journal:
        monitor = Monitor(journal, start)
        monitor.record(sample(), start)
    with Journal(path, scope()) as journal:
        monitor = Monitor(journal, start+timedelta(hours=4))
        monitor.record(sample(), start+timedelta(hours=4))
        kinds = [row['kind'] for row in journal.rows]
        assert kinds.count('start') == kinds.count('four_hour') == 1
        assert 'gap' in kinds
        assert len([r for r in journal.rows if r['kind'] == 'sample']) == 2
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='scope'):
        Journal(path, {**scope(), 'revision': 'different'})


def test_truncated_log_is_not_silently_repaired_or_accepted(tmp_path):
    from app.readiness_monitor import Journal
    path = tmp_path / 'broken.jsonl'
    path.write_text('{"kind":"sample"')
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        Journal(path, scope())
    assert path.read_text() == '{"kind":"sample"'

    import os
    with Journal(tmp_path/'full.jsonl', scope()) as journal:
        monkeypatch.setattr(os, 'fsync', lambda *_: (_ for _ in ()).throw(OSError('disk full')))
        with pytest.raises(OSError):
            journal.append('sample', datetime.now(timezone.utc), sample())
        assert journal.rows == []

    from app.readiness_observation import sample_database
    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _close, _headers

            assert logged['source'] == 'read_only_database' and logged['backlog_high'] == 1
            assert logged['conditions'] == ['workers_missing']
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_persistent_429_cap_recheck_and_counter_reset_are_logged(tmp_path):
    from app.readiness_monitor import Journal, Monitor
    now = datetime(2026, 9, 14, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    with Journal(tmp_path/'thresholds.jsonl', scope()) as journal:

        for second in range(0, 3601, 15):
            monitor.record({**sample(), 'returned_429': second*6, 'returned_total': second*10,
                            'settled_total': second*10, 'submitted_total': second*10,
                            'reserved_over_420': 1, 'over_cap_ids': [77]}, now+timedelta(seconds=second))
        codes = [r['data']['code'] for r in journal.rows if r['kind'] == 'stop_condition']
        assert codes.count('high_429_four_buckets') == 1
        assert codes.count('wait_over_420_confirmed') == 1
        confirmation = next(r for r in journal.rows if r.get('data', {}).get('code') == 'wait_over_420_confirmed')
            # 日志时间戳走平台时区编码（+08:00），同一瞬间、不同表示。
        assert confirmation['occurred_at'] == platform_isoformat(now+timedelta(seconds=60))
        monitor.record(sample(), now+timedelta(seconds=3615))
        assert journal.rows[-1]['data']['code'] == 'counter_reset'


def test_journal_export_needs_inventory_audit_and_detects_tampering(tmp_path):
    from app.readiness_monitor import Journal, Monitor, export_window
    from app.new_server_acceptance import assess_window
    now = datetime(2026, 9, 14, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    path = tmp_path/'export.jsonl'
    with Journal(path, scope()) as journal:
        monitor = Monitor(journal, now)
        monitor.record(sample(), now)
        monitor.record(sample(), now+timedelta(hours=4))
        exported = export_window(journal, start=now, end=now+timedelta(hours=4), items=[])
        assert 'receipts' not in exported and exported['inventory_complete'] is False
        result = assess_window(**exported)
        assert not result['accepted']
        assert {'coverage_gap', 'inventory_incomplete', 'logged_gap'} <= set(result['blockers'])
        assert 'event_not_persisted' not in result['blockers']
        with pytest.raises(BlockingIOError):
            Journal(path, scope())
    rows = path.read_text().replace('"sample_count":1', '"sample_count":3')
    path.write_text(rows)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='chain'):
        Journal(path, scope())


def test_db_carry_in_invocation_remains_in_settled_counter_after_completion(monkeypatch):
    from app.readiness_observation import sample_database
    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _close, _headers
    from test_incremental_sla import intake
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation, ProductionStepAttempt
    from sqlalchemy import select
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        run = intake(client, db, asset, _headers(monkeypatch), 1)
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        observed_scope = {**scope(), 'workloads': [dict(category_key='space_image',
            workflow_definition_id=run.workflow_definition_id)]}
        invocation = WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run.id, node_instance_id='prompt_a',
            step_attempt_id=db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id)),
            invocation_key='root', provider='doubao', model_id='fixture',
            status='reserved', estimated_cost_micros=12, created_at=now-timedelta(hours=2))
        db.add(invocation)
        db.commit()
        invocation_id = invocation.id
        db.rollback()
        first = sample_database(db, scope=observed_scope, start=now-timedelta(hours=1), now=now)
        assert first['reserved'] == 1 and first['submitted_total'] == 0
        invocation = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id)
        invocation.status = 'succeeded'
        invocation.finished_at = now
        invocation.actual_cost_micros = 3
        invocation.input_tokens = 1
        invocation.output_tokens = 1
        invocation.evidence_json = '{"result":{}}'
        db.commit()
        second = sample_database(db, scope=observed_scope, start=now-timedelta(hours=1), now=now+timedelta(seconds=15))
        assert second['settled_total'] == 1 and second['reserved'] == 0
        assert second['responsibility_micros'] == 3
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_export_cli_has_no_receipt_or_server_dependency(tmp_path):
    import subprocess
    import sys
    from pathlib import Path
    from app.readiness_monitor import Journal, Monitor
    now = datetime(2026, 9, 14, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    path, items = tmp_path/'cli.jsonl', tmp_path/'items.json'
    items.write_text('[]')
    with Journal(path, scope()) as journal:
        Monitor(journal, now).record(sample(), now)
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(root/'scripts/export-readiness-window.py'),
        '--journal', str(path), '--items', str(items), '--start', now.isoformat(),
        '--end', (now+timedelta(hours=4)).isoformat(), '--evidence-kind', 'synthetic'],
        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr
    data = json.loads(result.stdout)
    assert data['evidence_kind'] == 'synthetic' and data['inventory_complete'] is False
    assert data['events'][0]['kind'] == 'start' and 'receipts' not in data
    assert len(data['samples']) == 1
    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(root/'scripts/observe-new-server.py'), '--help'],
        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
    assert result.returncode == 0 and '--database-name' in result.stdout
    assert '--notify' not in result.stdout and '--deploy' not in result.stdout


def test_observer_connection_uses_utc_and_one_consistent_read_snapshot():
    from app.readiness_observation import configure_observer_connection
    statements = []
    class Connection:
        def cursor(self): return self
        def execute(self, sql): statements.append(sql)
        def close(self): statements.append('closed')
    configure_observer_connection(Connection())
    assert statements == ["SET SESSION time_zone = '+00:00'",
        'SET SESSION TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ', 'closed']

===== FILE backend/tests/test_readonly_source_resolver.py =====
"""上游只读取数适配器解析器的回归。

背景：``/api/upstream-source-contracts/{id}/poll`` 此前直接返回 503
``SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE``，而机制（合同、游标、分页、只读校验、读取运行
记录）都已完整，只差「从合同拿到真实连接」这一步。本文件锁定接上之后的行为。

和下游影子投影的解析器是同一形状、共用 ``external_datasources``，所以这里锁的
安全口径也一致：

1. **连接串与物理表名都不从合同里取。** 合同的 ``connection_locator`` 只是逻辑
   引用，真实 DSN 与表名配在部署侧。用**不同的物理列名**建表，以此证明字段映射
   真的在起作用（而不是碰巧同名）。
2. **只读是数据库层强制的。** SQLite 的 ``PRAGMA query_only`` 会被真正打开，
   所以适配器的只读探测拿到的是真实 True，写操作会被数据库拒绝。
3. **schema 指纹从活库实算。** 传合同登记值会让漂移门禁自证同一、形同废除。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base
from app.external_datasources import (
    ExternalDatasourceError,
    compute_live_schema_fingerprint,
    readonly_connection_factory,
    reset_engine_cache,
    resolve_locator_table,
)
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    UpstreamSourceContract,
)
from app.readonly_sources import (
    ReadOnlySourceError,
    SourceCursor,
    create_upstream_source_contract,
    poll_upstream_source,
    resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter,
)

#: 合同里存的逻辑引用（不是 DSN）。
_LOCATOR = "source-registry:fixture-3d"
_DSN_ENV = "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_SOURCE_REGISTRY_FIXTURE_3D"
_TABLE_ENV = _DSN_ENV + "_TABLE"
_TABLE = "dw_content_events"

#: 物理列名故意与逻辑字段名**都不一样**，用来证明字段映射真的生效。
_FIELD_MAPPINGS = {
    "content_id": "ll_content_id",
    "source_version": "ver",
    "category_key": "cat",
    "occurred_at": "happened_at",
    "asset_id": "material_id",
}

_SOURCE_DDL = f"""
CREATE TABLE {_TABLE} (
    ll_content_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    ver TEXT NOT NULL,
    cat TEXT NOT NULL,
    happened_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    material_id INTEGER
)
"""

_ROWS = [
    ("ll-100001", "v1", "space_image", "2026-08-24T10:00:00+00:00", 9001),
    ("ll-100002", "v1", "space_image", "2026-08-24T10:05:00+00:00", 9002),
    ("ll-100003", "v1", "space_image", "2026-08-24T10:10:00+00:00", None),
]


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clean_engine_cache():
    reset_engine_cache()
    yield
    reset_engine_cache()


@pytest.fixture
def upstream(tmp_path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> str:
    """建一个真实的上游库并配好部署侧变量，返回**逻辑引用**。"""
    path = tmp_path / "upstream.db"
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path}")
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(text(_SOURCE_DDL))
        for row in _ROWS:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    f"INSERT INTO {_TABLE}"
                    " (ll_content_id, ver, cat, happened_at, material_id)"
                    " VALUES (:c, :v, :k, :t, :a)"
                ),
                {"c": row[0], "v": row[1], "k": row[2], "t": row[3], "a": row[4]},
            )
    engine.dispose()
    reset_engine_cache()
    monkeypatch.setenv(_DSN_ENV, f"sqlite:///{path}")
    monkeypatch.setenv(_TABLE_ENV, _TABLE)
    return _LOCATOR


@pytest.fixture
def db() -> Session:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.close()
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.fixture
def local_prerequisites(db: Session) -> None:
    """建出 poll 落库所需的两项本地前提。

    ``poll_upstream_source`` 对每一行都会调 ``ingest_content_event``，那里有两道
    要求，缺任一整批都会被判 ``SOURCE_READ_FAILED``：

    1. **类目必须已启用**——需要一条 ``status="active"`` 的
       ``EvaluationCategoryProfile``。上游事件要落到已启用的类目上，否则后续的
       评测路由无从挂靠。
    2. **``asset_id`` 必须能反查到本地素材**——非空 asset_id 查不到就拒绝整批。
    """
    db.add(
        EvaluationCategoryProfile(
            category_key="space_image",
            display_name="空间图",
            status="active",
        )
    )
    db.add_all(
        Asset(
            id=asset_id,
            original_name=f"upstream-{asset_id}.png",
            stored_name=f"upstream-{asset_id}.png",
            mime_type="image/png",
            size_bytes=256,
            sha256=f"{asset_id:064d}",
            category_key="space_image",
        )
        for asset_id in (9001, 9002)
    )
    db.flush()


def _live_fingerprint(locator: str) -> str:
    return compute_live_schema_fingerprint(locator, None, table_name=_TABLE)


def _contract(
    db: Session,
    locator: str,
    *,
    fingerprint: str,
    adapter_key: str = "sql",
    read_only: bool = True,
    status: str = "active",
    field_mappings: dict[str, str] | None = None,
) -> UpstreamSourceContract:
    return create_upstream_source_contract(
        db,
        contract_key="dw-content-events",
        adapter_key=adapter_key,
        source_system="fixture-3d",
        category_key="space_image",
        connection_locator=locator,
        secret_reference="secret-ref:fixture-3d-readonly",
        field_mappings=field_mappings or dict(_FIELD_MAPPINGS),
        cursor_definition={"fields": ["content_id", "source_version"]},
        page_size=100,
        read_only=read_only,
        schema_fingerprint=fingerprint,
        owner="tpeng-3d",
        status=status,
        created_by="admin",
    )


# --- 1. 真的能读到数据 -----------------------------------------------------


def test_resolved_adapter_reads_rows_through_field_mappings(
    db: Session, upstream: str
) -> None:
    """取数走通，且物理列名经映射还原成逻辑字段。

    物理列叫 ``ll_content_id``/``ver``/``cat``/``happened_at``/``material_id``，
    逻辑字段是 ``content_id`` 等——能对上就说明映射真的生效了。
    """
    adapter = resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(
        _contract(db, upstream, fingerprint=_live_fingerprint(upstream))
    )
    page = adapter.fetch_page(cursor=None, limit=10)

    assert [row.content_id for row in page.rows] == [
        "ll-100001",
        "ll-100002",
        "ll-100003",
    ]
    first = page.rows[0]
    assert first.source_version == "v1"
    assert first.category_key == "space_image"
    assert first.asset_id == 9001
    assert first.occurred_at.isoformat() == "2026-08-24T10:00:00+00:00"
    # 空值列要如实还原成 None，不能变成 0。
    assert page.rows[2].asset_id is None


def test_cursor_pagination_advances_without_gaps_or_repeats(
    db: Session, upstream: str
) -> None:
    adapter = resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(
        _contract(db, upstream, fingerprint=_live_fingerprint(upstream))
    )
    first = adapter.fetch_page(cursor=None, limit=2)
    assert [row.content_id for row in first.rows] == ["ll-100001", "ll-100002"]

    second = adapter.fetch_page(cursor=first.next_cursor, limit=2)
    assert [row.content_id for row in second.rows] == ["ll-100003"]

    third = adapter.fetch_page(cursor=second.next_cursor, limit=2)
    assert third.rows == ()


# --- 2. 只读是数据库层强制的 -----------------------------------------------


def test_read_only_evidence_is_genuinely_true(db: Session, upstream: str) -> None:
    """只读证据必须是真实探测出来的。

    连接层会打开 SQLite 的 ``PRAGMA query_only``，所以适配器的探测拿到真 True。
    若只靠「退出时回滚」而不在数据库层设只读，这里会是 False，poll 会被门禁拦住。
    """
    adapter = resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(
        _contract(db, upstream, fingerprint=_live_fingerprint(upstream))
    )
    assert adapter.verify_read_only().read_only is True


def test_write_through_readonly_connection_is_rejected(upstream: str) -> None:
    """经只读连接写入必须被**数据库**拒绝，而不是先写成功再回滚。"""
    factory = readonly_connection_factory(upstream, None)
    with pytest.raises(Exception) as excinfo:
        with factory() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(f"INSERT INTO {_TABLE} (ll_content_id, ver, cat, happened_at)"
                     " VALUES ('x', 'v', 'k', '2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00')")
            )
    assert "readonly" in str(excinfo.value).lower() or "read-only" in str(
        excinfo.value
    ).lower()


def test_readonly_connection_leaves_no_trace(upstream: str) -> None:
    """只读连接用完后，上游数据行数不变。"""
    factory = readonly_connection_factory(upstream, None)
    with factory() as connection:
        before = connection.execute(text(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {_TABLE}")).scalar()
    reset_engine_cache()
    verifier = create_engine(os.environ[_DSN_ENV])
    with verifier.connect() as connection:
        after = connection.execute(text(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {_TABLE}")).scalar()
    verifier.dispose()
    assert before == after == len(_ROWS)


# --- 3. 指纹从活库实算 -----------------------------------------------------


def test_fingerprint_comes_from_live_schema_not_contract(
    db: Session, upstream: str
) -> None:
    """合同登记一个错误指纹，适配器给出的必须是活库真值。

    否则 poll 的漂移比对会永远相等、彻底失效。
    """
    wrong = "0" * 64
    adapter = resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(
        _contract(db, upstream, fingerprint=wrong)
    )
    page = adapter.fetch_page(cursor=None, limit=1)
    assert page.schema_fingerprint == _live_fingerprint(upstream)
    assert page.schema_fingerprint != wrong


def test_poll_detects_schema_drift(db: Session, upstream: str) -> None:
    """登记后上游表结构变化，poll 必须报漂移而不是照常取数。"""
    contract = _contract(db, upstream, fingerprint=_live_fingerprint(upstream))
    engine = create_engine(os.environ[_DSN_ENV])
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(text(f"ALTER TABLE {_TABLE} ADD COLUMN extra TEXT"))
    engine.dispose()
    reset_engine_cache()

    adapter = resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(contract)
    # poll 不抛异常，而是把这次运行标成 blocked 并记下错误码——运维要能在运行
    # 记录里看到「为什么没取数」，而不是只收到一个 500。
    run = poll_upstream_source(
        db, contract=contract, adapter=adapter, limit=10, actor="tester"
    )
    assert run.status == "blocked"
    assert run.error_code == "SOURCE_SCHEMA_DRIFT"


# --- 4. poll 端到端 --------------------------------------------------------


def test_poll_records_run_and_returns_rows(
    db: Session, upstream: str, local_prerequisites: None
) -> None:
    contract = _contract(db, upstream, fingerprint=_live_fingerprint(upstream))
    adapter = resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(contract)
    run = poll_upstream_source(
        db, contract=contract, adapter=adapter, limit=10, actor="tester"
    )
    assert run.row_count == len(_ROWS)
    assert run.source_contract_id == contract.id
    assert run.status == "succeeded"


def test_poll_resumes_from_cursor(
    db: Session, upstream: str, local_prerequisites: None
) -> None:
    contract = _contract(db, upstream, fingerprint=_live_fingerprint(upstream))
    adapter = resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(contract)
    run = poll_upstream_source(
        db,
        contract=contract,
        adapter=adapter,
        limit=10,
        actor="tester",
        cursor=SourceCursor({"content_id": "ll-100001", "source_version": "v1"}),
    )
    assert run.row_count == len(_ROWS) - 1


# --- 5. 拒绝不该解析的合同 -------------------------------------------------


def test_non_read_only_contract_is_rejected(upstream: str) -> None:
    """未声明只读的合同必须被拒。

    用未落库对象来测——建合同时 ``read_only=False`` 可能被数据库约束挡住，
    而解析器本身也必须复核，不盲信传进来的合同。
    """
    contract = UpstreamSourceContract(
        contract_key="not-read-only",
        adapter_key="sql",
        source_system="fixture-3d",
        category_key="space_image",
        connection_locator=upstream,
        secret_reference="secret-ref:x",
        field_mappings_json="{}",
        cursor_definition_json="{}",
        page_size=100,
        read_only=False,
        schema_fingerprint="0" * 64,
        owner="tpeng-3d",
        status="active",
        created_by="admin",
    )
    with pytest.raises(ReadOnlySourceError) as excinfo:
        resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(contract)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "SOURCE_CONTRACT_NOT_READ_ONLY"


def test_non_sql_adapter_key_is_rejected(db: Session, upstream: str) -> None:
    contract = _contract(
        db,
        upstream,
        fingerprint=_live_fingerprint(upstream),
        adapter_key="fixture-readonly",
    )
    with pytest.raises(ReadOnlySourceError) as excinfo:
        resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(contract)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE"


def test_unconfigured_table_fails_closed(
    db: Session, upstream: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    """部署侧没配物理表名时必须报错并指明该设哪个变量。"""
    contract = _contract(db, upstream, fingerprint=_live_fingerprint(upstream))
    monkeypatch.delenv(_TABLE_ENV, raising=False)
    with pytest.raises(ReadOnlySourceError) as excinfo:
        resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(contract)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE"
    assert _TABLE_ENV in str(excinfo.value)


def test_unconfigured_dsn_fails_closed(
    db: Session, upstream: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    contract = _contract(db, upstream, fingerprint=_live_fingerprint(upstream))
    monkeypatch.delenv(_DSN_ENV, raising=False)
    with pytest.raises(ReadOnlySourceError) as excinfo:
        resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(contract)
    assert _DSN_ENV in str(excinfo.value)


def test_missing_table_in_live_db_is_rejected(
    db: Session, upstream: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    """配的表在上游库里不存在时必须拒绝，而不是解析出必然取数失败的适配器。"""
    contract = _contract(db, upstream, fingerprint=_live_fingerprint(upstream))
    monkeypatch.setenv(_TABLE_ENV, "no_such_table")
    with pytest.raises(ReadOnlySourceError) as excinfo:
        resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(contract)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE"


def test_locator_table_resolution_rejects_injection(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """部署侧配了非法表名也要拒——表名会拼进 SQL。"""
    monkeypatch.setenv(_TABLE_ENV, 'x"; DROP TABLE y;--')
    with pytest.raises(ExternalDatasourceError) as excinfo:
        resolve_locator_table(_LOCATOR)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "DATASOURCE_TABLE_INVALID"

def test_sql_adapter_rechecks_read_only_on_fetch_connection() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            """
            CREATE TABLE source_assets (
                content_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                source_version TEXT NOT NULL,
                category_key TEXT NOT NULL,
                occurred_at TEXT NOT NULL
            )
            """
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            """
            INSERT INTO source_assets (
                content_id, source_version, category_key, occurred_at
            ) VALUES ('1001', 'v1', 'space_image', '2026-08-14T00:00:00+00:00')
            """
        )

    connection_count = 0

    @contextmanager
    def connection_factory() -> Iterator[object]:
        nonlocal connection_count
        connection_count += 1
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "PRAGMA query_only = ON"
                if connection_count == 1
                else "PRAGMA query_only = OFF"
            )
            yield connection

    adapter = SqlReadOnlySourceAdapter(
        connection_factory=connection_factory,
        table_name="source_assets",
        field_mappings={
            "content_id": "content_id",
            "source_version": "source_version",
            "category_key": "category_key",
            "occurred_at": "occurred_at",
        },
        schema_fingerprint=_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT,
    )
    try:
        assert adapter.verify_read_only().read_only is True
        with pytest.raises(ReadOnlySourceError, match="只读"):
            adapter.fetch_page(cursor=None, limit=10)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _activate_incremental_route(db: Session) -> Asset:
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="source-3d.png",
        stored_name="source-3d.png",
        mime_type="image/png",
        size_bytes=256,
        sha256="a" * 64,
        category_key="space_image",
    )
    contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    contract["category_key"] = "space_image"
    db.add_all(
        [
            asset,
            CategoryEvaluationV3Config(
                category_key="space_image",
                display_name="空间图",
                status="active",
                contract_json=json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False),
                classification_map_json=json.dumps(
                    build_inspiration_classification_map(), ensure_ascii=False
                ),
                subcategory_dimensions_json=json.dumps(
                    build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions(), ensure_ascii=False
                ),
                contract_hash=canonical_contract_hash(contract),
                created_by="test",
            ),
        ]
    )
    workflow_version = seed_incremental_versions(
        db,
        category_key="space_image",
        count=1,
    )[0]
    set_incremental_mechanism(
        db,
        category_key="space_image",
        workflow_version_id=workflow_version.id,
        expected_lock_version=None,
        reason="只读数据源测试启用",
        actor="test",
    )
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(asset)
    return asset


def test_replaying_source_page_reuses_event_asset_version_and_package(
    db: Session,
    source_contract: UpstreamSourceContract,
) -> None:
    asset = _activate_incremental_route(db)
    row = SourceRow(
        content_id="1001",
        source_version="v7",
        category_key="space_image",
        occurred_at=datetime(2026, 8, 14, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        asset_id=asset.id,
    )
    adapter = FixtureReadOnlySourceAdapter(
        read_only=True,
        rows=[row],
        schema_fingerprint=_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT,
    )

    first = poll_upstream_source(
        db,
        contract=source_contract,
        adapter=adapter,
        limit=10,
        actor="admin",
    )
    second = poll_upstream_source(
        db,
        contract=source_contract,
        adapter=adapter,
        limit=10,
        actor="admin",
    )
    db.commit()

    assert first.status == "succeeded"
    assert second.status == "succeeded"
    assert first.package_count == 1
    assert second.duplicate_count == 1
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ContentIngressEvent.id))) == 1
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(AssetVersion.id))) == 1
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(MaterialPackage.id))) == 1
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(UpstreamReadRun.id))) == 2


def test_invalid_row_rolls_back_the_entire_source_page(
    db: Session,
    source_contract: UpstreamSourceContract,
) -> None:
    asset = _activate_incremental_route(db)
    occurred_at = datetime(2026, 8, 14, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

            ),

    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(AssetVersion.id))) == 0
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(MaterialPackage.id))) == 0


@contextmanager
def _client_context(
    sessions: sessionmaker[Session],
    *,
    role: str,
    is_admin: bool = False,
) -> Iterator[TestClient]:
    user = User(
        username=f"{role}-source-user",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name=role,
        is_admin=is_admin,
        role=role,
    )

    def override_db() -> Iterator[Session]:
        with sessions() as session:
            yield session

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        with TestClient(app) as client:
            yield client
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()


def test_source_contract_api_is_admin_written_and_never_exposes_secret_value(
    sessions: sessionmaker[Session],
) -> None:
    with _client_context(sessions, role="admin", is_admin=True) as client:
        created = client.post("/api/upstream-source-contracts", json=_contract_args())
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        assert created.json()["version"] == 1
        assert created.json()["secret_reference"] == "secret-ref:fixture-3d-readonly"
        assert created.json()["secret_status"] == "unresolved"

    with _client_context(sessions, role="viewer") as client:
        denied = client.post("/api/upstream-source-contracts", json=_contract_args())
        listed = client.get("/api/upstream-source-contracts")
        runs = client.get("/api/upstream-read-runs")
        assert denied.status_code == 403
        assert listed.status_code == 200
        assert runs.status_code == 200
        serialized = listed.text.lower()
        assert "postgres://" not in serialized
        assert "password=" not in serialized
        assert "secret_value" not in serialized

===== FILE backend/tests/test_recommendation_calibration.py =====
"""No network/database: frozen visual boundary, caps and provenance are real inputs."""
from copy import deepcopy
import json

import pytest

from app.recommendation_calibration import (
    FEATURE_VERSION, PROFILE_KEY, SCHEMA, apply_calibration, digest,
    evidence_features, manifest_profile, mechanism_bindings, probability, validate_profile,
)


def fixture(weight=8):
    matcher = dict(version="category-level-scale-v1", levels=[
        dict(level=f"L{i}", enabled=i < 5, min_score=minimum)
        for i, minimum in enumerate([81, 61, 41, 0, None], 1)
    ])
    nodes = [dict(node_instance_id=key, kind="prompt", config=dict(asset_key=key,
        component_snapshot=dict(content_hash=digest(key)),
        model_snapshot=dict(provider="local_qwen", snapshot_hash=digest("model"))))
        for key in ("prompt_a", "prompt_b")]
    nodes.append(dict(node_instance_id="matcher", kind="rule_module", config=dict(
        module_type_key="score_level_matcher", component_snapshot=dict(content_json=json.dumps(matcher)))))
    manifest = dict(category_key="model_3d_su", nodes=nodes)
    profile = dict(schema_version=SCHEMA, feature_version=FEATURE_VERSION, threshold=.505,
        features=[["bias", weight, None]], bindings=mechanism_bindings(manifest),
        training=dict(run_id=446, rows=2210, source_sha256="a"*64,
                      purpose="golden_calibration", minimum_document_frequency=40))
    matcher[PROFILE_KEY] = profile
    nodes[-1]["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_json"] = json.dumps(matcher)
    return profile, matcher, manifest


def scoring(score=60, level="L3", **changes):
    return dict(score=score, level=level, raw_level=level, caps=[], hard_reject=False,
        engine_version="original-engine", steps=[], **changes)


def test_recommendation_crosses_actual_frozen_boundary_and_preserves_raw_facts():
    profile, matcher, manifest = fixture()
    old = scoring()
    a, b = {"classification": {"evidence": ["材质层次清晰"]}}, {"aesthetic_score": 60}
    before = deepcopy((a, b, old, manifest))
    result = apply_calibration(old, profile=profile, matcher=matcher, precheck=a, dimensions=b)
    assert (result["score"], result["level"]) == (61, "L2")  # Not the global default of 60.
    assert result["raw_level"] == "L3"
    assert result["recommendation_calibration"]["original_score"] == 60
    assert result["recommendation_calibration"]["profile_sha256"] == digest(profile)
    assert result["engine_version"] != old["engine_version"]
    assert result["steps"][-1]["score_after"] == 61
    assert (a, b, old, manifest) == before
    assert manifest_profile(manifest) == (profile, matcher)


def test_negative_boundary_preserves_regular_subgrade_and_consistent_score():
    profile, matcher, _ = fixture(-8)
    for old, expected in [(scoring(85, "L1"), (60, "L3")), (scoring(25, "L4"), (25, "L4"))]:
        result = apply_calibration(old, profile=profile, matcher=matcher, precheck={}, dimensions={"aesthetic_score": 85})
        assert (result["score"], result["level"]) == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize("change", [
    {"hard_reject": True}, {"caps": [{"rule": "veto"}]}, {"hard_defect_action": "cap"},
    {"needs_review": True}, {"level": "L5"}, {"score": None, "level": None},
])
def test_protected_results_are_byte_identical(change):
    profile, matcher, _ = fixture()
    old = scoring(); old.update(change)
    result = apply_calibration(old, profile=profile, matcher=matcher, precheck={}, dimensions={"aesthetic_score": 85})
    assert result is old
    assert apply_calibration(scoring(), profile=profile, matcher=matcher, precheck={}, dimensions={}) == scoring()


def test_identity_title_truth_and_ascii_cannot_change_features_or_probability():
    profile, _, _ = fixture(0)
    profile["features"].append(["text/b_evidence/材质", 2, 1.8])
    a, b, n = {"production_fields": {"title": "标题甲", "cons": ""}}, {"aesthetic_evidence": ["材质真实"]}, scoring()
    original = evidence_features(a, b, n); first = probability(profile, a, b, n)
    for obj in (a, b, n):
        obj.update(asset_id=1, filename="abc.jpg", truth_level="L1", image_hash="f"*64, golden_membership=True)
    a["production_fields"].update(title="另一个标题", seotitle="黄金答案", asset_id=9001)
    b["aesthetic_evidence"].append("L1 asset_id=9001 /file/abc.jpg " + "f"*64)
    assert evidence_features(a, b, n) == original
    assert probability(profile, a, b, n) == first
    b["aesthetic_evidence"] = ["光影平淡"]
    assert probability(profile, a, b, n) < first


@pytest.mark.parametrize("change", [
    lambda p: p.update(threshold=float("nan")), lambda p: p.update(threshold=True),
    lambda p: p["features"].append(["asset_id/1", 1, None]),
    lambda p: p["features"].append(["text/b_evidence/独有特征", 1, 2]),
    lambda p: p["features"].append(["text/b_evidence/材质", 1, None]),
    lambda p: p["features"].append(["bias", 1, None]),
    lambda p: p["features"][0].__setitem__(1, float("inf")),
    lambda p: p["training"].update(minimum_document_frequency=1),
    lambda p: p.update(asset_truth={"1": "L1"}),
])
def test_invalid_or_memorizing_profiles_fail_closed(change):
    profile, _, _ = fixture(); change(profile)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError): validate_profile(profile)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("change", [
    lambda m: m.update(category_key="inspiration_image"),
    lambda m: m["nodes"][0]["config"].update(temperature=.7),
    lambda m: m["nodes"][1]["config"]["model_snapshot"].update(provider="doubao"),
    lambda m: m["nodes"].append(deepcopy(m["nodes"][-1])),
])
def test_other_category_model_prompt_or_duplicate_matcher_cannot_reuse_profile(change):
    _, _, manifest = fixture(); change(manifest)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError): manifest_profile(manifest)


def test_changing_scoring_rules_requires_a_new_bound_profile():
    _, matcher, manifest = fixture()
    matcher["levels"][1]["min_score"] = 60
    manifest["nodes"][-1]["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_json"] = json.dumps(matcher)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="binding_mismatch"): manifest_profile(manifest)
